    cached_apply = pd.to_datetime(events.first_apply_time, errors="coerce") if "first_apply_time" in events else pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index)
    cached_sources = pd.concat([old_mother, october], ignore_index=True).set_index("event_id")
    def cached_column(name):
        # Keep original stored boundaries, including the old October 7 cutoff.
        # The original September labels can contain an October-support next T0
        # even though the saved formal mother itself stops at September 30.
        if name in events:
            return pd.to_datetime(events[name], errors="coerce")
        if name in cached_sources:
            return pd.to_datetime(events.event_id.map(cached_sources[name]), errors="coerce")
        return pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    cached_next = cached_column("next_t0_time")
    cached_cutoff = cached_column("data_cutoff_ts")
    cached_followup = cached_column("followup_end")
    events["next_t0_time"] = events.event_id.map(next_by_id)
    orders = timestamps(orders, ["apply_time"])
    required = ["user_id", "serial_id", "apply_time"]
    if not set(required).issubset(orders):
        raise ValueError(f"Order extract lacks {required}")
    if orders["user_id"].isna().any() or orders["apply_time"].isna().any():
        raise ValueError("Order extract has missing user_id or apply_time")
    if orders.duplicated(["user_id", "serial_id"]).any():
        raise ValueError("Order extract duplicates user/order identity")
    missing_users = set(events.user_id) - set(orders.user_id)
    if missing_users:
        raise ValueError(f"Historical order extract omits {len(missing_users)} scored users")
    followup = pd.Series(CUTOFF, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    for cap in [events.next_t0_time, events.as_of_time.dt.normalize() + pd.Timedelta(days=16)]:
        mask = cap.notna() & (cap < followup)
        followup.loc[mask] = cap.loc[mask]
    apply = pd.Series(pd.NaT, index=events.index, dtype="datetime64[ns]")
    event_groups = events.groupby("user_id", sort=False).groups
    examined = 0
    for uid, og in orders.groupby("user_id", sort=False):
        if uid not in event_groups:
            continue
        ix = event_groups[uid]
        a = np.sort(og.apply_time.to_numpy("datetime64[ns]"))
        t = events.loc[ix, "as_of_time"].to_numpy("datetime64[ns]")
        where = np.searchsorted(a, t, side="left")
        valid = where < len(a)
        positions = np.flatnonzero(valid)
        if len(positions):
            candidate = a[where[positions]]
            before_end = candidate < followup.loc[ix].to_numpy("datetime64[ns]")[positions]
            chosen = np.asarray(ix)[positions[before_end]]
            apply.loc[chosen] = candidate[before_end]
        examined += len(ix)
    if examined != len(events):
        raise AssertionError("Not all event user histories were examined")
    equals = (cached_apply == apply) | (cached_apply.isna() & apply.isna())
    october_mask = events.as_of_time >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-01")
    changed = ~equals
    added = cached_apply.isna() & apply.notna()
    removed = cached_apply.notna() & apply.isna()
    moved = cached_apply.notna() & apply.notna() & (cached_apply != apply)
    next_cutoff_removed = removed & events.next_t0_time.notna() & (cached_apply >= events.next_t0_time)
    category = pd.Series("unchanged", index=events.index, dtype="string")
    category.loc[added] = "newly_observed_application"
    category.loc[next_cutoff_removed] = "old_application_removed_by_reconstructed_next_t0_boundary"
    category.loc[removed & ~next_cutoff_removed] = "old_application_removed_other_reason"
    category.loc[moved] = "application_time_changed_both_observed"
    category.loc[changed & category.eq("unchanged")] = "other_change"
    added_after_original = added & cached_cutoff.notna() & (apply >= cached_cutoff)
    added_before_original = added & cached_cutoff.notna() & (apply < cached_cutoff)
    next_boundary_changed = ~((cached_next == events.next_t0_time) | (cached_next.isna() & events.next_t0_time.isna()))
    change_rows = pd.DataFrame({
        "event_id": events.event_id, "event_time": events.as_of_time,
        "period": np.where(october_mask, "october", "september_saved"),
        "change_category": category, "cached_first_apply_time": cached_apply,
        "rebuilt_first_apply_time": apply, "cached_next_t0_time": cached_next,
        "rebuilt_next_t0_time": events.next_t0_time,
        "cached_data_cutoff_ts": cached_cutoff, "cached_followup_end": cached_followup,
        "rebuilt_followup_end": followup,
        "new_hit_at_or_after_original_cutoff": added_after_original,
        "new_hit_before_original_cutoff": added_before_original,
        "new_hit_at_or_after_20261007": added & (apply >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-07")),
        "next_t0_boundary_changed": next_boundary_changed,
    }).loc[changed].copy()
    if len(change_rows) != int(changed.sum()) or int(category.ne("unchanged").sum()) != int(changed.sum()):
        raise AssertionError("Changed-label audit categories do not reconcile")
    if change_audit_path is not None:
        change_audit_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # No user IDs, input contents, bank/phone details or other sensitive
        # attributes; this is an event/time audit and never HTML body content.
        change_rows.to_csv(change_audit_path, index=False)
    events["first_apply_time"] = apply
    events["followup_end"] = followup
    events["data_cutoff_ts"] = CUTOFF
    audit = {"combined_mother_events": len(mother), "orders": len(orders), "order_users": orders.user_id.nunique(),
             "scored_users_missing_from_order_history": len(missing_users),
             "rebuilt_first_apply_total_mismatch_vs_cached": int((~equals).sum()),
             "rebuilt_october_first_apply_mismatch_vs_cached": int((~equals & october_mask).sum()),
             "first_apply_change_categories": {k: int(category.eq(k).sum()) for k in [
                 "newly_observed_application", "old_application_removed_by_reconstructed_next_t0_boundary",
                 "old_application_removed_other_reason", "application_time_changed_both_observed", "other_change"]},
             "newly_observed_application": {
                 "count": int(added.sum()),
                 "at_or_after_original_row_cutoff": int(added_after_original.sum()),
                 "before_original_row_cutoff": int(added_before_original.sum()),
                 "original_row_cutoff_unknown": int((added & cached_cutoff.isna()).sum()),
                 "at_or_after_20261007": int((added & (apply >= pd.Timestamp("2026-10-07"))).sum()),
                 "before_20261007": int((added & (apply < pd.Timestamp("2026-10-07"))).sum()),
                 "first_new_application_time": str(apply.loc[added].min()) if added.any() else None,
                 "last_new_application_time": str(apply.loc[added].max()) if added.any() else None,
             },
             "removed_by_next_t0_boundary": {
                 "count": int(next_cutoff_removed.sum()),
                 "boundary_changed_from_cached": int((next_cutoff_removed & next_boundary_changed).sum()),
                 "old_hit_before_new_next_t0": int((next_cutoff_removed & (cached_apply < events.next_t0_time)).sum()),
             },
             "change_audit_csv": str(change_audit_path.resolve()) if change_audit_path is not None else None,
             "change_audit_sha256": digest(change_audit_path) if change_audit_path is not None else None,
             "matched_application_events": int(apply.notna().sum()),
             "next_t0_definition": "LEAD(as_of_time) by user from original Jan-Sep strict/dedup T0 mother plus October strict/dedup T0 mother, using unchanged original event_id",
             "first_apply_definition": "Earliest all-order apply_time >= T0 and < min(next T0, cutoff, offer date +16days), without requiring successful funding or same credit order",
             "cutoff_exclusive": str(CUTOFF), "raw_event_id_preserved": True}
    return events, audit


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--features", type=Path, default=ROOT / "features")
    parser.add_argument("--orders", type=Path, default=ROOT / "sources/orders.csv.gz")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=ROOT / "events_predictions.parquet")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    sep, sep_audit, mother = old_september()
    october, oct_audit = score_october(args.features)
    common = list(dict.fromkeys(["event_id", "user_id", "as_of_time", "vir_date", "first_apply_time", "next_t0_time", "followup_end", "data_cutoff_ts", "credit_serial_id", "settled_serial_id",
        "successful_loan_count", "product_count", "risk_product", "credit_direction", "risk_score_405", "risk_score_363",
        "cyc_ever_eligible", "cyc_ever_applied", "prediction_source", "prediction_time_status"] +
        [f"p_{m}_d{h}" for m in ["v3", "v4"] for h in HORIZONS]))
    assembled = pd.concat([sep.reindex(columns=common), october.reindex(columns=common)], ignore_index=True)
    id_check(assembled, "Assembled September-October scores")
    assembled = assembled.sort_values(["as_of_time", "event_id"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    assembled, label_audit = rebuild_observation(assembled, mother, october, pd.read_csv(args.orders), args.out.parent / "label_changes_audit.csv")
    # Product count must come from the original product set rather than a current
    # user snapshot. This is the original frozen model's set parsing policy.
    tokens = definitions(V4 / "product_features.py", ["tokens"], {})["tokens"]
    parsed_count = assembled.risk_product.map(lambda s: len(tokens(s)))
    existing = pd.to_numeric(assembled.product_count, errors="coerce")
    conflict = existing.notna() & existing.ne(parsed_count)
    if conflict.any():
        raise ValueError("Stored product count differs from frozen product-set parser")
    assembled["product_count"] = parsed_count
    applied = pd.to_numeric(assembled.cyc_ever_applied, errors="coerce")
    eligible = pd.to_numeric(assembled.cyc_ever_eligible, errors="coerce")
    assembled["seg_history_cycle"] = np.select([applied.gt(0), eligible.gt(0), eligible.eq(0) & applied.eq(0)],
        ["历史循环贷曾提单", "曾获循环贷资格但未提单", "无循环贷资格历史"], default="历史特征缺失")
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if args.out.suffix=='.parquet':
        assembled.to_parquet(args.out, index=False)
    else:
        # The frozen numpy-2 model runtime need not include a parquet engine.
        # CSV uses full float repr; conversion elsewhere uses round_trip parsing.
        assembled.to_csv(args.out, index=False, compression='infer')
    audit = {"september": sep_audit, "october": oct_audit, "labels": label_audit,
             "total_events": len(assembled), "total_users": assembled.user_id.nunique(),
             "prediction_sources": assembled.prediction_source.value_counts().to_dict(),
             "output_path": str(args.out.resolve()), "output_sha256": digest(args.out),
             "orders_path": str(args.orders.resolve()), "orders_sha256": digest(args.orders),
             "segment_definitions": {"seg_history_cycle": "Original frozen cyc_ever_eligible/cyc_ever_applied computed using histories observable strictly before each T0, not current user cycle eligibility.",
                 "successful_loan_count": "Original cumulative successfully funded orders before each T0; grouping performed by evaluator.",
                 "product_count": "Distinct nonempty normalized tokens of current T0 risk_product, using frozen product_features.tokens()."},
             "no_database_access": True, "no_model_training": True, "no_original_artifact_writes": True}
    # pandas/numpy integer values from group counts are made portable explicitly.
    (args.out.parent / "score_audit.json").write_text(json.dumps(audit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=lambda x: int(x) if isinstance(x, np.integer) else str(x)))
    print(json.dumps({"output": str(args.out), "events": len(assembled), "users": int(assembled.user_id.nunique()), "labels": label_audit}, default=str))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/evaluate_validation.py
"""Evaluate frozen T0 predictions on fully mature event cohorts, without training.

Clock contract: all naive timestamps are America/Belize local time. Aware
timestamps are converted to this zone and made naive. End boundaries are
exclusive. Labels are reconstructed here rather than trusting cached y_d*.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HORIZONS = (0, 3, 7, 15)
CUTOFF = pd.Timestamp("2026-10-25 00:00:00")
TZ = "America/Belize"
COHORTS = {
    "main": (pd.Timestamp("2026-10-09"), CUTOFF),
    "supplemental": (pd.Timestamp("2026-09-22"), pd.Timestamp("2026-10-09")),
}
METRICS = ["auc", "pr_ap", "ks", "brier", "logloss", "actual_rate", "mean_prediction"] + [
    f"top{k}_{suffix}" for k in (5, 10, 20) for suffix in ("precision", "recall", "lift")
]


def local_time(series: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
    out = pd.to_datetime(series, errors="coerce", format="mixed")
    if isinstance(out.dtype, pd.DatetimeTZDtype):
        out = out.dt.tz_convert(TZ).dt.tz_localize(None)
    if not pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(out.dtype):
        raise ValueError(f"{series.name}: mixed aware/naive timestamps are not accepted")
    return out


def prepare_labels(events: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Require chronological maturity BEFORE permitting an early positive label."""
    df = events.copy()
    if "next_t0_time" not in df and "next_t0" in df:
        df = df.rename(columns={"next_t0": "next_t0_time"})
    required = ["event_id", "user_id", "as_of_time", "next_t0_time", "first_apply_time"]
    missing = [c for c in required if c not in df]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"Missing input columns: {missing}")
    if df.event_id.isna().any() or df.event_id.duplicated().any():
        raise ValueError("event_id must be non-null and globally unique")
    if df.user_id.isna().any():
        raise ValueError("user_id must not be missing")
    for c in ["as_of_time", "next_t0_time", "first_apply_time", "followup_end", "data_cutoff_ts"]:
        if c in df:
            original_nonnull = df[c].notna()
            df[c] = local_time(df[c])
            if (original_nonnull & df[c].isna()).any():
                raise ValueError(f"Unparseable timestamp in {c}")
    if df.as_of_time.isna().any():
        raise ValueError("as_of_time must be present")
    if (df.next_t0_time.notna() & (df.next_t0_time <= df.as_of_time)).any():
        raise ValueError("next_t0_time must be strictly later than as_of_time")
    if (df.first_apply_time.notna() & (df.first_apply_time < df.as_of_time)).any():
        raise ValueError("first_apply_time must not precede the event")
    df["offer_date"] = df.as_of_time.dt.normalize()
    df["offer_week"] = (df.offer_date - pd.to_timedelta(df.offer_date.dt.dayofweek, unit="D")).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    # These are administrative observation limits, not last-seen activity times.
    df["administrative_cutoff"] = CUTOFF
    if "data_cutoff_ts" in df:
        mask = df.data_cutoff_ts.notna() & (df.data_cutoff_ts < CUTOFF)
        df.loc[mask, "administrative_cutoff"] = df.loc[mask, "data_cutoff_ts"]
    if (df.administrative_cutoff <= df.as_of_time).any():
        raise ValueError("Observation cutoff must be later than every scored event")
    df["effective_followup_end"] = df.administrative_cutoff
    for c in ["next_t0_time", "followup_end"]:
        if c in df:
            mask = df[c].notna() & (df[c] < df.effective_followup_end)
            df.loc[mask, "effective_followup_end"] = df.loc[mask, c]
    if (df.effective_followup_end <= df.as_of_time).any():
        raise ValueError("Follow-up end must be later than every event")
    apply_after_next = df.first_apply_time.notna() & df.next_t0_time.notna() & (df.first_apply_time >= df.next_t0_time)
    apply_after_admin = df.first_apply_time.notna() & (df.first_apply_time >= df.administrative_cutoff)
    # Do not let a later T0's application leak backwards to the prior event.
    valid_hit_time = df.first_apply_time.notna() & (df.first_apply_time < df.effective_followup_end)
    audit: dict[str, Any] = {
        "input_events": len(df), "input_users": df.user_id.nunique(),
        "first_apply_at_or_after_next_t0_ignored": int(apply_after_next.sum()),
        "first_apply_at_or_after_administrative_cutoff_ignored": int(apply_after_admin.sum()),
        "probability_nonfinite_or_missing": {}, "probability_monotonicity_violations": {},
    }
    for h in HORIZONS:
        boundary = df.offer_date + pd.Timedelta(days=h + 1)
        mature = boundary <= df.administrative_cutoff
        hit = valid_hit_time & (df.first_apply_time < boundary)
        complete = df.effective_followup_end >= boundary
        df[f"boundary_d{h}"] = boundary
        df[f"mature_d{h}"] = mature
        df[f"censored_d{h}"] = mature & ~hit & ~complete
        df[f"y_d{h}"] = np.where(mature & hit, 1.0, np.where(mature & complete, 0.0, np.nan))
        for model in ("v4", "v3"):
            c = f"p_{model}_d{h}"
            if c not in df:
                df[c] = np.nan
            values = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce")
            if ((values < 0) | (values > 1)).any():
                raise ValueError(f"Probability outside [0,1] in {c}")
            values = values.where(np.isfinite(values))
            df[c] = values
            audit["probability_nonfinite_or_missing"][c] = int(values.isna().sum())
    for model in ("v4", "v3"):
        matrix = df[[f"p_{model}_d{h}" for h in HORIZONS]].to_numpy(float)
        bad = np.isfinite(matrix[:, :-1]) & np.isfinite(matrix[:, 1:]) & (matrix[:, :-1] > matrix[:, 1:] + 1e-10)
        audit["probability_monotonicity_violations"][model] = int(np.any(bad, axis=1).sum())
        if audit["probability_monotonicity_violations"][model]:
            raise ValueError(f"Non-monotone cumulative predictions for {model}")
    # Segments are snapshots at T0; no future state/current loan count is permitted.
    if "successful_loan_count" in df and "seg_loan_count" not in df:
        n = pd.to_numeric(df.successful_loan_count, errors="coerce")
        df["seg_loan_count"] = pd.cut(n, [0, 1, 3, 5, 10, np.inf], labels=["1次", "2—3次", "4—5次", "6—10次", "11次及以上"]).astype("string").fillna("缺失/非正次数")
    if "product_count" in df and "seg_product_count" not in df:
        n = pd.to_numeric(df.product_count, errors="coerce")
        df["seg_product_count"] = np.select([n.eq(1), n.eq(2), n.gt(2)], ["单产品", "双产品", "三产品及以上"], default="缺失/无产品")
    if "credit_direction" in df and "seg_credit_direction" not in df:
        n = pd.to_numeric(df.credit_direction, errors="coerce")
        df["seg_credit_direction"] = np.select([n.gt(0), n.eq(0), n.lt(0)], ["提额", "额度不变", "降额"], default="缺失")
    for c in ["risk_405_group", "risk_363_group", "history_cycle_group", "activity_group"]:
        if c in df and f"seg_{c}" not in df:
            df[f"seg_{c}"] = df[c].astype("string").fillna("缺失")
    return df, audit


class SortedMetrics:
    """Fixed score order, weighted tie groups, reusable in cluster bootstrap."""
    def __init__(self, y: np.ndarray, p: np.ndarray):
        self.y = np.asarray(y, float)
        self.p = np.asarray(p, float)
        if len(y) == 0:
            self.order = np.array([], dtype=int)
            self.starts = np.array([], dtype=int)
            self.scores = np.array([], dtype=float)
        else:
            self.order = np.argsort(-self.p, kind="stable")
            sorted_p = self.p[self.order]
            self.starts = np.r_[0, 1 + np.flatnonzero(sorted_p[1:] != sorted_p[:-1])]
            self.scores = sorted_p[self.starts]
        clipped = np.clip(self.p, 1e-15, 1 - 1e-15)
        self.squared_error = (self.p - self.y) ** 2
        self.cross_entropy = -(self.y * np.log(clipped) + (1 - self.y) * np.log1p(-clipped))

    def groups(self, weights: np.ndarray | None = None) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        if weights is None:
            weights = np.ones(len(self.y))
        sw = weights[self.order]
        return np.add.reduceat(sw, self.starts), np.add.reduceat(sw * self.y[self.order], self.starts)

    @staticmethod
    def band(cw: np.ndarray, gp: np.ndarray, lower: float, upper: float) -> tuple[float, float, np.ndarray]:
        before = np.r_[0.0, cw[:-1]]
        mass = cw - before
        overlap = np.maximum(0, np.minimum(cw, upper) - np.maximum(before, lower))
        fractions = np.divide(overlap, mass, out=np.zeros_like(overlap), where=mass > 0)
        return float(overlap.sum()), float(np.dot(fractions, gp)), fractions

    def compute(self, weights: np.ndarray | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
        if len(self.y) == 0:
            return {k: np.nan for k in METRICS}
        if weights is None:
            weights = np.ones(len(self.y))
        n = float(weights.sum())
        if n <= 0:
            return {k: np.nan for k in METRICS}
        group_n, group_y = self.groups(weights)
        cw = np.cumsum(group_n)
        tp = np.cumsum(group_y)
        positives = float(tp[-1])
        negatives = n - positives
        result = {"actual_rate": positives / n,
                  "mean_prediction": float(np.dot(weights, self.p) / n),
                  "brier": float(np.dot(weights, self.squared_error) / n),
                  "logloss": float(np.dot(weights, self.cross_entropy) / n)}
        if positives > 0:
            precision = np.divide(tp, cw, out=np.zeros_like(tp), where=cw > 0)
            result["pr_ap"] = float(np.dot(precision, group_y) / positives)
        else:
            result["pr_ap"] = np.nan
        if positives > 0 and negatives > 0:
            tpr = np.r_[0, tp / positives]
            fpr = np.r_[0, (cw - tp) / negatives]
            result["auc"] = float(np.sum((tpr[1:] + tpr[:-1]) * np.diff(fpr) / 2))
            result["ks"] = float(np.max(tpr - fpr))
        else:
            result["auc"] = result["ks"] = np.nan
        for k in (5, 10, 20):
            selected_n, selected_y, _ = self.band(cw, group_y, 0, n * k / 100)
            rate = selected_y / selected_n if selected_n else np.nan
            result[f"top{k}_precision"] = rate
            result[f"top{k}_recall"] = selected_y / positives if positives else np.nan
            result[f"top{k}_lift"] = rate / result["actual_rate"] if positives else np.nan
        return result

    def deciles(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if len(self.y) == 0:
            return []
        gn, gy = self.groups()
        cw = np.cumsum(gn)
        n = float(cw[-1])
        total_y = float(gy.sum())
        result = []
        cumulative_y = 0.0
        for d in range(10):
            count, positives, fractions = self.band(cw, gy, n * d / 10, n * (d + 1) / 10)
            used = fractions > 0
            predicted = float(np.dot(fractions * gn, self.scores) / count)
            actual = positives / count
            cumulative_y += positives
            result.append({"decile": d + 1, "event_weight": count, "positive_weight": positives,
                           "actual_rate": actual, "mean_prediction": predicted,
                           "calibration_gap": predicted - actual,
                           "score_max": float(self.scores[used].max()), "score_min": float(self.scores[used].min()),
                           "coverage": positives / total_y if total_y else np.nan,
                           "cumulative_coverage": cumulative_y / total_y if total_y else np.nan,
                           "lift": actual / (total_y / n) if total_y else np.nan})
        return result


def summary(frame: pd.DataFrame, model: str, h: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pcol, ycol = f"p_{model}_d{h}", f"y_d{h}"
    g = frame[frame[ycol].notna() & frame[pcol].notna()]
    score = SortedMetrics(g[ycol].to_numpy(float), g[pcol].to_numpy(float))
    metrics = score.compute()
    rate = metrics["actual_rate"]
    constant_brier = rate * (1 - rate)
    constant_logloss = -(rate * np.log(rate) + (1 - rate) * np.log1p(-rate)) if 0 < rate < 1 else (0.0 if np.isfinite(rate) else np.nan)
    return {"n": len(g), "users": g.user_id.nunique(), "positives": int(g[ycol].sum()),
            "status": "ok" if len(g) else "no_evaluable_scores", **metrics,
            "constant_brier": constant_brier, "constant_logloss": constant_logloss,
            "brier_skill": 1 - metrics["brier"] / constant_brier if constant_brier > 0 else np.nan}


def bootstrap_intervals(frame: pd.DataFrame, h: int, n_boot: int, seed: int, paired: bool) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    models = ("v4", "v3") if paired else ("v4",)
    mask = frame[f"y_d{h}"].notna()
    for model in models:
        mask &= frame[f"p_{model}_d{h}"].notna()
    g = frame[mask]
    if len(g) == 0 or n_boot <= 0:
        return []
    y = g[f"y_d{h}"].to_numpy(float)
    prepared = {m: SortedMetrics(y, g[f"p_{m}_d{h}"].to_numpy(float)) for m in models}
    point = {m: obj.compute() for m, obj in prepared.items()}
    cluster, users = pd.factorize(g.user_id, sort=True)
    n_users = len(users)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    storage = {m: {metric: [] for metric in METRICS} for m in models}
    if paired:
        storage["v4_minus_v3"] = {metric: [] for metric in METRICS}
    for _ in range(n_boot):
        # Resample users, carrying all their T0 events. The same resample is used
        # for both models; repeated users weight their complete event histories.
        cluster_counts = np.bincount(rng.integers(0, n_users, size=n_users), minlength=n_users)
        weights = cluster_counts[cluster].astype(float)
        metrics = {m: obj.compute(weights) for m, obj in prepared.items()}
        for m in models:
            for metric in METRICS:
                storage[m][metric].append(metrics[m][metric])
        if paired:
            for metric in METRICS:
                storage["v4_minus_v3"][metric].append(metrics["v4"][metric] - metrics["v3"][metric])
    rows = []
    for comparison, values in storage.items():
        for metric, sample in values.items():
            sample = np.asarray(sample)
            sample = sample[np.isfinite(sample)]
            estimate = point["v4"][metric] - point["v3"][metric] if comparison == "v4_minus_v3" else point[comparison][metric]
            rows.append({"comparison": comparison, "metric": metric, "estimate": estimate,
                         "ci_low": float(np.quantile(sample, .025)) if len(sample) else np.nan,
                         "ci_high": float(np.quantile(sample, .975)) if len(sample) else np.nan,
                         "n_boot_valid": len(sample), "n_boot_requested": n_boot, "n": len(g), "users": n_users,
                         "sample": "paired" if paired else "v4_available", "seed": seed})
    return rows


def evaluate(events: pd.DataFrame, n_boot: int = 500, seed: int = 20261025) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], pd.DataFrame]:
    df, audit = prepare_labels(events)
    result: dict[str, Any] = {k: [] for k in ["overall", "flow", "weekly", "deciles", "calibration", "subgroups", "pairwise", "confidence_intervals"]}
    for ci, (cohort, (start, end)) in enumerate(COHORTS.items()):
        cohort_df = df[(df.as_of_time >= start) & (df.as_of_time < end)]
        for hi, h in enumerate(HORIZONS):
            ycol = f"y_d{h}"
            mature = cohort_df[f"mature_d{h}"]
            known = cohort_df[ycol].notna()
            scored = known & cohort_df[f"p_v4_d{h}"].notna()
            paired = scored & cohort_df[f"p_v3_d{h}"].notna()
            mature_df = cohort_df[mature]
            key = {"cohort": cohort, "horizon": h}
            result["flow"].append({**key, "total_events": len(cohort_df), "total_users": cohort_df.user_id.nunique(),
                "mature_events": int(mature.sum()), "immature_events": int((~mature).sum()),
                "censored_events": int(cohort_df[f"censored_d{h}"].sum()),
                "censored_without_hit": int(cohort_df[f"censored_d{h}"].sum()),
                "evaluable_events": int(known.sum()), "positives": int(cohort_df[ycol].sum()),
                "scored_v4_events": int(scored.sum()), "paired_events": int(paired.sum()),
                "first_offer_date": str(mature_df.offer_date.min().date()) if len(mature_df) else None,
                "last_offer_date": str(mature_df.offer_date.max().date()) if len(mature_df) else None})
            for model in ("v4", "v3"):
                if not cohort_df[f"p_{model}_d{h}"].notna().any():
                    continue
                result["overall"].append({**key, "model": model, **summary(cohort_df, model, h)})
                g = cohort_df[known & cohort_df[f"p_{model}_d{h}"].notna()]
                score = SortedMetrics(g[ycol].to_numpy(float), g[f"p_{model}_d{h}"].to_numpy(float))
                dec = [{**key, "model": model, **row} for row in score.deciles()]
                result["deciles"].extend(dec)
                result["calibration"].extend(dec)
                for week, wg in cohort_df.groupby("offer_week", dropna=False):
                    result["weekly"].append({**key, "model": model, "week": week, "total_events": len(wg),
                        "mature_events": int(wg[f"mature_d{h}"].sum()), "censored_events": int(wg[f"censored_d{h}"].sum()),
                        **summary(wg, model, h)})
                for segment in [c for c in df if c.startswith("seg_")]:
                    for value, sg in cohort_df.groupby(segment, dropna=False):
                        result["subgroups"].append({**key, "model": model, "grouping": segment, "group": str(value),
                            "total_events": len(sg), "mature_events": int(sg[f"mature_d{h}"].sum()), **summary(sg, model, h)})
            paired_df = cohort_df[paired]
            if len(paired_df):
                new, old = summary(paired_df, "v4", h), summary(paired_df, "v3", h)
                result["pairwise"].append({**key, "n": len(paired_df), "users": paired_df.user_id.nunique(),
                    **{f"v4_{m}": new[m] for m in METRICS}, **{f"v3_{m}": old[m] for m in METRICS},
                    **{f"delta_{m}": new[m] - old[m] for m in METRICS}})
            base_seed = seed + ci * 100 + hi * 10
            intervals = bootstrap_intervals(cohort_df, h, n_boot, base_seed, paired=False)
            intervals += bootstrap_intervals(cohort_df, h, n_boot, base_seed + 1, paired=True)
            result["confidence_intervals"].extend([{**key, **row} for row in intervals])
    result["meta"] = {
        "timezone": TZ, "cutoff_exclusive": str(CUTOFF), "bootstrap_replicates": n_boot, "bootstrap_seed": seed,
        "cohorts": {c: {"start_inclusive": str(a), "end_exclusive": str(b)} for c, (a, b) in COHORTS.items()},
        "maturity_latest_offer_dates": {str(h): str((CUTOFF - pd.Timedelta(days=h+1)).date()) for h in HORIZONS},
        "maturity": "First require offer-date + (h+1) natural days <= administrative cutoff; early positives from immature events are excluded.",
        "label": "First user application at/after T0, strictly before next T0, administrative cutoff and horizon end; no application plus complete followup is 0; otherwise censored/unknown.",
        "metric_unit": "T0 event; users may have repeated events. Events, not users, form Top-% lists.",
        "top_ties": "Exact probability ties share inclusion weight equally at each rank boundary; Top-x% uses expected fractional event/positive counts with budget x% * N. No outcome or ID breaks ties.",
        "decile_ties": "Ten equal expected-size score-ranked bins, 1=highest; tied events crossing a boundary split proportionally. Event_weight/positive_weight may be noninteger.",
        "ci": "95% percentile user-cluster bootstrap; resample users with replacement and carry all user events. V4-V3 uses identical paired events and resamples. No multiplicity correction; exploratory subgroup estimates.",
        "pr_ap": "Average precision, sum(score-threshold precision * positive recall increment); not trapezoidal PR AUC.",
        "calibration_gap": "mean_prediction - actual_rate, positive means overprediction",
        "constant_reference": "constant_brier=actual_rate*(1-actual_rate); constant_logloss=entropy(actual_rate); brier_skill=1-model_brier/constant_brier. Outcome-fitted constant on the same evaluation sample is a calibration diagnostic, not a deployable prior or tuning baseline.",
        "grouping_definitions": {
            "seg_loan_count": "Successful loan count available at T0, grouped 1 / 2-3 / 4-5 / 6-10 / 11+; missing/nonpositive separately; no future lifecycle count is used.",
            "seg_product_count": "Number of distinct products in the T0 approved risk_product set: one / two / three or more; missing or empty separately.",
            "seg_credit_direction": "Sign of the current T0 approved credit amount minus previous approved credit amount; rise / unchanged / fall / missing.",
            "seg_history_cycle": "Frozen original cyc_ever_eligible/cyc_ever_applied features computed using strictly prior cycle-loan credit offers and observed applications at T0: ever applied / eligible but never applied / no eligible history; missing kept separate.",
        },
        "audit": audit,
    }
    result["limitations"] = [
        "This is a retrospective replay unless timestamped original scores and feature snapshots are independently established.",
        "Fully mature then evaluable events are assessed; next-T0 censoring can be informative. Complete-case metrics do not identify performance among censored events.",
        "The main cohort D15 only has October 9 mature; it cannot establish temporal stability.",
        "Supplemental dates precede the claimed model completion date and are diagnostic, not independent post-freeze verification.",
        "Actual label availability and historical feature availability must be audited separately; reconstructed features cannot prove online point-in-time availability.",
        "Top-percent metrics measure association/prediction. They are not evidence that recall operations cause incremental applications or profit.",
        "Subgroup and weekly small-sample/single-class metrics are reported as missing where undefined; confidence intervals describe sampling uncertainty conditional on observed data.",
    ]
    return result, df


def json_safe(obj: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {str(k): json_safe(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [json_safe(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):
        return int(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, (float, np.floating)):
        return float(obj) if np.isfinite(obj) else None
    if isinstance(obj, (pd.Timestamp,)):
        return str(obj)
    return obj


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--bootstrap", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=20261025)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    events = pd.read_parquet(args.input)
    results, labeled = evaluate(events, args.bootstrap, args.seed)
    results["meta"]["input_path"] = str(args.input.resolve())
    results["meta"]["input_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(args.input.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (args.out / "metrics.json").write_text(json.dumps(json_safe(results), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    for name in ["overall", "flow", "weekly", "deciles", "calibration", "subgroups", "pairwise", "confidence_intervals"]:
        pd.DataFrame(results[name]).to_csv(args.out / f"{name}.csv", index=False)
    labeled.to_parquet(args.out / "labels_evaluated.parquet", index=False)
    print(json.dumps({"output": str(args.out), "events": len(labeled), "overall_rows": len(results["overall"]), "bootstrap": args.bootstrap}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
t0_offer_strategy_20261025/analyze_model_targeting.py
"""Frozen T0 V4 targeting replay: D3 probability selection vs D0 non-application.

No training, no database access, no threshold optimization. All times are local
America/Belize and the outcome cutoff is 2026-10-25 00:00:00 exclusive.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

os.environ.setdefault("OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "2")
os.environ.setdefault("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "2")
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
VALIDATION = ROOT.parent / "t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025"
BOOTSTRAP = 500
SEED = 20261025
RATES = {
    "selection_coverage": ("selected_events", "cohort_events"),
    "ppv_no_d0": ("selected_d0_noapply", "selected_d0_known"),
    "d0_false_offer_rate": ("selected_d0_apply", "selected_d0_known"),
    "npv_d3": ("selected_d3_noapply", "selected_d3_known"),
    "d3_false_nonapply_rate": ("selected_d3_apply", "selected_d3_known"),
    "baseline_no_d0": ("cohort_d0_noapply", "cohort_d0_known"),
    "baseline_no_d3": ("cohort_d3_noapply", "cohort_d3_known"),
    "d0_noapply_capture": ("selected_d0_noapply", "cohort_d0_noapply"),
    "d1_3_natural_apply_share": ("selected_d1_3_apply", "selected_d3_known"),
    "d3_nonapply_capture": ("selected_d3_noapply", "cohort_d3_noapply"),
}


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def clean(v):
    if isinstance(v, dict):
        return {str(k): clean(x) for k, x in v.items()}
    if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
        return [clean(x) for x in v]
    if isinstance(v, np.integer):
        return int(v)
    if isinstance(v, (np.floating, float)):
        return float(v) if np.isfinite(v) else None
    if isinstance(v, (pd.Timestamp, np.datetime64)):
        return str(v)
    return v


def count_vectors(df, selected):
    """Columns are additive event counts; allows exact user cluster bootstrap."""
    n = len(df)
    s = np.asarray(selected, float)
    y0 = df.y_d0.to_numpy(float)
    y3 = df.y_d3.to_numpy(float)
    z0, o0, z3, o3 = y0 == 0, y0 == 1, y3 == 0, y3 == 1
    return {
        "cohort_events": np.ones(n),
        "cohort_d0_known": (z0 | o0).astype(float),
        "cohort_d0_noapply": z0.astype(float),
        "cohort_d0_apply": o0.astype(float),
        "cohort_d3_known": (z3 | o3).astype(float),
        "cohort_d3_noapply": z3.astype(float),
        "cohort_d3_apply": o3.astype(float),
        "selected_events": s,
        "selected_d0_known": s * (z0 | o0),
        "selected_d0_noapply": s * z0,
        "selected_d0_apply": s * o0,
        "selected_d3_known": s * (z3 | o3),
        "selected_d3_noapply": s * z3,
        "selected_d3_apply": s * o3,
        "selected_d1_3_apply": s * z0 * o3,
        # D3 confusion matrix: predicted application is probability >= threshold.
        "d3_tn": s * z3,
        "d3_fn": s * o3,
        "d3_fp": (1-s) * z3,
        "d3_tp": (1-s) * o3,
        # D0 decision confusion matrix: target=actual no application today.
        "d0_target_tp": s * z0,
        "d0_target_fp": s * o0,
        "d0_target_fn": (1-s) * z0,
        "d0_target_tn": (1-s) * o0,
    }


def rates(counts):
    r = {}
    for name, (a, b) in RATES.items():
        r[name] = np.divide(counts[a], counts[b], out=np.full_like(np.asarray(counts[b], float), np.nan), where=np.asarray(counts[b]) > 0)
    r["d0_nonapply_lift_pp"] = 100 * (r["ppv_no_d0"] - r["baseline_no_d0"])
    r["d3_nonapply_lift_pp"] = 100 * (r["npv_d3"] - r["baseline_no_d3"])
    return r


def lowest_weights(p, k):
    """Random allocation in an exact score tie, expressed as expected quota."""
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    if k == 0:
        return np.zeros(len(p))
    if k == len(p):
        return np.ones(len(p))
    boundary = np.partition(p, k-1)[k-1]
    below = p < boundary
    tied = p == boundary
    weights = below.astype(float)
    weights[tied] = (k - below.sum()) / tied.sum()
    assert abs(weights.sum() - k) < 1e-7
    return weights


def analyze_cohort(df, cohort_name, seed):
    """Replay prespecified probability thresholds and descriptive low-score caps."""
    df = df.sort_values(["p_v4_d3", "event_id"], kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)
    if not len(df):
        raise ValueError(f"Empty cohort {cohort_name}")
    codes, users = pd.factorize(df.user_id, sort=True)
    u = len(users)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # Same resampled users under every rule and the baseline enables paired CIs.
    boot_weights = rng.multinomial(u, np.full(u, 1/u), size=BOOTSTRAP)
    specs = [("probability_threshold", f"p_d3<{t:.1f}", float(t), None, df.p_v4_d3.to_numpy() < t)
             for t in [i / 10 for i in range(1, 10)]]
    for pct in range(10, 100, 10):
        k = int(np.floor(len(df) * pct / 100))
        specs.append(("lowest_probability_share", f"lowest_d3_{pct}%", None, pct, lowest_weights(df.p_v4_d3.to_numpy(), k)))
    # D0 direct ranking at the same volume as the prespecified D3 0.5 rule.
    k = int((df.p_v4_d3 < .5).sum())
    match0 = lowest_weights(df.p_v4_d0.to_numpy(), k)
    specs.append(("direct_d0_same_volume_diagnostic", "lowest_d0_same_volume_as_d3_lt_0.5", None, None, match0))
    for d0_threshold in (.1, .2, .3):
        gate = (df.p_v4_d3.to_numpy() < .5) & (df.p_v4_d0.to_numpy() < d0_threshold)
        specs.append(("combined_d3_and_d0_threshold_diagnostic", f"p_d3<0.5_and_p_d0<{d0_threshold:.1f}", .5, None, gate))
    out = []
    main_selection = None
    for kind, name, threshold, pct, selected in specs:
        cols = count_vectors(df, selected)
        keys = list(cols)
        mat = np.column_stack([cols[k] for k in keys])
        base = dict(zip(keys, mat.sum(axis=0)))
        point = rates(base)
        user_mat = np.column_stack([np.bincount(codes, weights=cols[k], minlength=u) for k in keys])
        bootstrap_counts_matrix = boot_weights @ user_mat
        boot_counts = {k: bootstrap_counts_matrix[:, i] for i, k in enumerate(keys)}
        boot_rates = rates(boot_counts)
        row = {"cohort": cohort_name, "rule_type": kind, "rule": name, "threshold": threshold,
               "requested_low_share_percent": pct,
               **{k: (int(round(v)) if abs(v-round(v)) < 1e-8 else float(v)) for k, v in base.items()},
               "cohort_users": u, "selected_users": int(df.loc[np.asarray(selected)>0, "user_id"].nunique()),
               "selected_users_count_status": "Exact" if set(np.unique(selected)).issubset({0, 1}) else "Users with any positive allocation; boundary tie event counts are expected quotas",
               "selected_min_p_d3": float(df.loc[np.asarray(selected)>0, "p_v4_d3"].min()) if selected.any() else None,
               "selected_max_p_d3": float(df.loc[np.asarray(selected)>0, "p_v4_d3"].max()) if selected.any() else None,
               "selected_mean_p_d0": float(np.average(df.p_v4_d0, weights=selected)) if selected.any() else None,
               "selected_mean_p_d3": float(np.average(df.p_v4_d3, weights=selected)) if selected.any() else None,
               **{k: float(v) for k, v in point.items()}}
        for metric, values in boot_rates.items():
            values = np.asarray(values, float)
            finite = values[np.isfinite(values)]
            row[f"{metric}_ci95_low"] = float(np.quantile(finite, .025)) if len(finite) else None
            row[f"{metric}_ci95_high"] = float(np.quantile(finite, .975)) if len(finite) else None
            row[f"{metric}_bootstrap_valid"] = len(finite)
        row["d0_no_observed_failures_sparse_warning"] = bool(base["selected_d0_apply"] == 0 and base["selected_d0_known"] > 0)
        if name == "p_d3<0.5":
            main_selection = df.assign(selected_d3_lt_0_5=selected)
        out.append(row)
    assert main_selection is not None
    return out, main_selection


def equal_volume_pair(df):
    """Paired D0-low vs D3-low comparison using the same user bootstrap draws."""
    df = df.sort_values(["p_v4_d3", "event_id"], kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)
    s3 = (df.p_v4_d3.to_numpy() < .5).astype(float)
    s0 = lowest_weights(df.p_v4_d0.to_numpy(), int(s3.sum()))
    codes, users = pd.factorize(df.user_id, sort=True)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    boots = rng.multinomial(len(users), np.full(len(users), 1/len(users)), size=BOOTSTRAP)
    rs = []
    for selection in (s3, s0):
        columns = count_vectors(df, selection)
        cc = {k: boots @ np.bincount(codes, weights=v, minlength=len(users)) for k, v in columns.items()}
        point = rates({k: v.sum() for k, v in columns.items()})
        bs = rates(cc)
        rs.append((point, bs))
    comparisons = {}
    for metric in ("ppv_no_d0", "d0_false_offer_rate", "npv_d3", "d1_3_natural_apply_share"):
        delta = 100 * (rs[1][1][metric] - rs[0][1][metric])
        comparisons[metric] = {
            "d0_rule_minus_d3_rule_pp": float(100*(rs[1][0][metric] - rs[0][0][metric])),
            "paired_cluster_ci95_low_pp": float(np.nanquantile(delta, .025)),
            "paired_cluster_ci95_high_pp": float(np.nanquantile(delta, .975)),
        }
    return {
        "cohort": "main_d3_mature_20261009_20261021", "equal_event_quota": int(s3.sum()),
        "tie_allocation": "Equal expected quota within boundary score ties, no outcome-based tie break",
        "comparisons": comparisons,
        "interpretation": "The D0-specific output aligns directly to avoiding same-day natural applications. Its observed same-volume gain must be judged with this paired interval and independently confirmed; it is not a proven incentive-uplift model.",
    }


def flow(df, requested, name):
    d = df[requested]
    return {"cohort": name, "events_requested": len(d), "users_requested": d.user_id.nunique(),
            "d0_mature_events": int(d.mature_d0.sum()), "d3_mature_events": int(d.mature_d3.sum()),
            "d0_known_events": int(d.y_d0.notna().sum()), "d3_known_events": int(d.y_d3.notna().sum()),
            "d0_censored_events": int(d.censored_d0.sum()), "d3_censored_events": int(d.censored_d3.sum()),
            "p_d3_missing_events": int(d.p_v4_d3.isna().sum()),
            "date_min": str(d.offer_date.min()), "date_max": str(d.offer_date.max())}


def main():
    source = VALIDATION / "events_predictions.parquet"
    labeled = VALIDATION / "evaluation/labels_evaluated.parquet"
    df = pd.read_parquet(labeled)
    events = pd.read_parquet(source)
    # Rebuild the labels with the frozen validation contract, not inferred silence.
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("old_validation", VALIDATION / "evaluate_validation.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    rebuilt, _ = module.prepare_labels(events)
    r = rebuilt.set_index("event_id").sort_index()
    s = df.set_index("event_id").sort_index()
    for c in ("y_d0", "y_d3", "mature_d0", "mature_d3", "censored_d0", "censored_d3"):
        np.testing.assert_equal(s[c].to_numpy(), r[c].to_numpy())
    assert not df.event_id.duplicated().any()
    assert not ((df.y_d0 == 1) & (df.y_d3 == 0)).any()
    primary_requested = (df.offer_date >= "2026-10-09") & (df.offer_date <= "2026-10-24")
    matured_requested = (df.offer_date >= "2026-10-09") & (df.offer_date <= "2026-10-21")
    supp_requested = (df.offer_date >= "2026-09-22") & (df.offer_date <= "2026-10-08")
    main_mask = matured_requested & df.mature_d3 & df.y_d3.notna() & df.p_v4_d3.notna()
    all_d0_mask = primary_requested & df.mature_d0 & df.y_d0.notna() & df.p_v4_d3.notna()
    sensitivity_mask = all_d0_mask & (df.offer_date != "2026-10-24")
    supplement_mask = supp_requested & df.mature_d3 & df.y_d3.notna() & df.p_v4_d3.notna()
    cohorts = {
        "main_d3_mature_20261009_20261021": main_mask,
        "supplement_d0_mature_20261009_20261024": all_d0_mask,
        "sensitivity_exclude_oct24_20261009_20261023": sensitivity_mask,
        "historical_supplement_20260922_20261008": supplement_mask,
    }
    all_rows = []
    selections = []
    for i, (name, mask) in enumerate(cohorts.items()):
        rows, selected = analyze_cohort(df.loc[mask].copy(), name, SEED + i)
        all_rows.extend(rows)
        selected["targeting_cohort"] = name
        selections.append(selected)
        headline = next(x for x in rows if x["rule"] == "p_d3<0.5")
        print(name, json.dumps(clean(headline), ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
    results = pd.DataFrame(all_rows)
    results.to_csv(ROOT / "model_targeting.csv", index=False)
    pd.concat(selections, ignore_index=True).to_parquet(ROOT / "model_targeting_event_replay.parquet", index=False)
    headline = {name: next(x for x in all_rows if x["cohort"] == name and x["rule"] == "p_d3<0.5") for name in cohorts}
    original = df.loc[matured_requested & df.mature_d3 & df.p_v4_d3.notna()].copy()
    censored = original[original.y_d3.isna()]
    censored_selected = censored[censored.p_v4_d3 < .5]
    flow_rows = [flow(df, primary_requested, "primary_requested_20261009_20261024"),
                 flow(df, matured_requested, "main_date_range_20261009_20261021"),
                 flow(df, supp_requested, "historical_supplement_20260922_20261008")]
    result = {
        "generated_utc": str(pd.Timestamp.now(tz="UTC")),
        "contract": {
            "model": "frozen T0 application timing V4",
            "d3_definition": "From passed T0 offer timestamp through the end of its third following calendar day; event_date + 4 days exclusive (D0-D3, four calendar dates). Not rolling 72 hours.",
            "d0_definition": "From the passed T0 offer timestamp to event_date + 1 day exclusive.",
            "label": "First actual user application, any serial ID; not loan disbursement.",
            "cutoff_exclusive": "2026-10-25 00:00:00 America/Belize",
            "selection": "p_v4_d3 < threshold; threshold equality belongs to predicted application.",
            "low_share_rules": "Exact floor(n * percentage / 100) expected event quota by ascending p_v4_d3; equal probabilities receive equal fractional selection weights at the boundary (no outcome-based tie break). Descriptive current-cohort capacities, not an optimized operational threshold.",
            "main_denominator": "2026-10-09 to 2026-10-21 events, D3 fully mature, D3 label known, score nonmissing. No conditioning on D0 outcome.",
            "censoring": "The next strict T0 censors this event; if it precedes D3 and no valid application has been observed, D3 label is unknown. These events are excluded from the main D3-label-known cohort and quantified separately.",
            "supplement": "All 2026-10-09 to 2026-10-24 D0 mature/known events. D3 metrics only use its D3 mature/known subset; late cohorts are not filled with negative labels.",
            "bootstrap": f"{BOOTSTRAP} percentile user-cluster bootstrap replicates, all repeated events for a user resampled together; paired baseline and rules. Fixed observed eligibility and selection; does not quantify censoring or model/source bias.",
            "seed": SEED,
            "threshold_status": "Prespecified descriptive grid; no selection of best threshold on this test set.",
            "oct24_sensitivity": "All 588 Oct24 events lacked source risk-model rows in prior audit. A separate D0 cohort excludes that date; the main D3 mature cohort never includes Oct24.",
            "evidence_level": "Retrospective replay from frozen models and reconstructed features; not a prospective randomized incentive or verified contemporaneous online-score trial.",
        },
        "source_files": {str(source): sha(source), str(labeled): sha(labeled)},
        "label_consistency_check": "All 22,365 event IDs and D0/D3 labels, maturity and censoring indicators match independent regeneration from the prior frozen evaluation code.",
        "flow": flow_rows,
        "main_d3_censored_exclusion": {
            "events": len(censored), "users": censored.user_id.nunique(),
            "selected_by_p_d3_lt_0_5_events": len(censored_selected),
            "selected_d0_known_events": int(censored_selected.y_d0.notna().sum()),
            "selected_d0_apply_events": int((censored_selected.y_d0 == 1).sum()),
            "selected_d0_noapply_events": int((censored_selected.y_d0 == 0).sum()),
        },
        "headlines_at_threshold_0_5": headline,
        "equal_volume_d0_vs_d3": equal_volume_pair(df.loc[main_mask].copy()),
        "results": all_rows,
        "operational_interpretation": [
            "D0 non-application precision measures avoidance of customers who would apply on the same day under observed historical conditions. It is not incentive uplift, profit uplift, or proof that the remaining users need a discount.",
            "D1-D3 natural applicants may also be subsidized or accelerated; include this economic leakage when comparing incremental profits.",
            "The observed usual policies are not an untreated counterfactual. The same-day leakage is an operational proxy under historical policy, not a causal estimate of wasted coupons.",
            "p(D3 application) < 0.5 does not mean guaranteed no application. Raising the threshold buys more coverage with lower precision; choose using an independently designed experiment and expected incremental profit.",
            "If the actual objective is only D0 non-application, direct p(D0) ranking at equal coverage is an aligned diagnostic; do not pick a winning targeting rule on this same replay without independent confirmation.",
            "At very low probability thresholds, an observed zero D0 failure count can make percentile cluster-bootstrap intervals degenerate at 100%. This does not prove 100% future precision; explicitly inspect selected counts and require larger prospective samples.",
        ],
    }
    (ROOT / "model_targeting.json").write_text(json.dumps(clean(result), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    pd.DataFrame(flow_rows).to_csv(ROOT / "model_targeting_flow.csv", index=False)
    print("WROTE", ROOT / "model_targeting.json", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
t0_risk410_lifecycle_strategy_20261025/analyze_prediction_profiles.py
"""Frozen low-pD3 targeting crossed with original-offer 410 and lifecycle profiles.

All candidate rules are descriptive. No training, threshold optimization, causal
uplift claim, or database access occurs here. Original validation files are read
only; unknown/censored labels are never filled as non-application.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import os
os.environ.setdefault('OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS','2')
os.environ.setdefault('OMP_NUM_THREADS','2')
from pathlib import Path
import argparse,hashlib,json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
OLD=ROOT.parent/'t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025'
SEED=20261025
REPLICATES=500
SCORE_BANDS=['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50','NO_RECORD','NULL_SCORE','INVALID','CONFLICT','UNMAPPED']
LIFE_BANDS=['1','2','3','4','5','6-10','11-20','21+','UNKNOWN','UNMAPPED']


def safe(x):
    if isinstance(x,dict):return {str(k):safe(v) for k,v in x.items()}
    if isinstance(x,(list,tuple)):return [safe(v) for v in x]
    if isinstance(x,np.integer):return int(x)
    if isinstance(x,(float,np.floating)):return float(x) if np.isfinite(x) else None
    if isinstance(x,(pd.Timestamp,np.datetime64)):return str(x)
    return x


def ratio(a,b):
    a,b=np.asarray(a,float),np.asarray(b,float)
    return np.divide(a,b,out=np.full_like(b,np.nan),where=b>0)


def json_write(path,data):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(safe(data),ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')


def sha(path):return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def local_time(s):
    x=pd.to_datetime(s,errors='coerce',format='mixed')
    if isinstance(x.dtype,pd.DatetimeTZDtype):x=x.dt.tz_convert('America/Belize').dt.tz_localize(None)
    return x


def add_profiles(events,mapping):
    required=['event_id','user_id','vir_time','score_410','score_status','score_band','settled_loan_seq','lifecycle_band']
    missing=[c for c in required if c not in mapping]
    if missing:raise ValueError(f'Mapping missing required columns: {missing}')
    if mapping.event_id.isna().any() or mapping.event_id.duplicated().any():raise ValueError('Mapping event_id must be unique and non-NULL')
    if mapping.user_id.isna().any():raise ValueError('Mapping user_id must be non-NULL')
    m=mapping.rename(columns={'user_id':'profile_user_id','vir_time':'profile_vir_time'})
    # Keep all profile columns, giving any existing validation columns precedence.
    data=events.merge(m,on='event_id',how='left',validate='one_to_one',indicator='_profile_join',suffixes=('','_profile'))
    found=data._profile_join.eq('both')
    user_bad=found & data.user_id.ne(data.profile_user_id)
    if user_bad.any():raise ValueError(f'{int(user_bad.sum())} event IDs map to different users; cannot use those profiles')
    data['profile_vir_time']=local_time(data.profile_vir_time)
    delta=(data.profile_vir_time-data.as_of_time).abs().dt.total_seconds()
    time_unknown=found & data.profile_vir_time.isna()
    time_bad=found & delta.gt(1)
    data['profile_mapped']=found & ~time_unknown & ~time_bad
    data['profile_key_status']=np.select([~found,time_unknown,time_bad],['UNMAPPED','MISSING_OFFER_TIME','OFFER_TIME_MISMATCH'],default='MATCHED')
    for c in ['score_band','lifecycle_band','score_status']:
        data[c]=data[c].astype('string').fillna('UNKNOWN')
        data.loc[~data.profile_mapped,c]='UNMAPPED'
    data['lifecycle_band']=data.lifecycle_band.str.replace('—','-',regex=False).str.replace('–','-',regex=False)
    invalid_bands=set(data.score_band.unique())-set(SCORE_BANDS)
    invalid_life=set(data.lifecycle_band.unique())-set(LIFE_BANDS)
    if invalid_bands:raise ValueError(f'Unexpected score_band values: {invalid_bands}')
    if invalid_life:raise ValueError(f'Unexpected lifecycle_band values: {invalid_life}')
    if 'score_time' in data:
        score_time=local_time(data.score_time)
        score_future=data.profile_mapped & score_time.notna() & score_time.gt(data.as_of_time)
        if score_future.any():raise ValueError(f'{int(score_future.sum())} profile scores are timestamped after the event')
        score_time_known=int((data.profile_mapped & score_time.notna()).sum())
    else:score_time_known=0
    quality={
      'events':len(data),'users':int(data.user_id.nunique()),'mapping_rows_input':len(mapping),
      'matched_event_id_events':int(found.sum()),'profile_mapped_events':int(data.profile_mapped.sum()),
      'unmatched_event_id_events':int((~found).sum()),'mapping_time_missing_events':int(time_unknown.sum()),
      'mapping_time_mismatch_over_one_second_events':int(time_bad.sum()),'mapping_user_mismatch_events':0,
      'profile_mapping_coverage':float(data.profile_mapped.mean()),'score_generation_time_known_events':score_time_known,
      'known_valid_score_events':int(data.score_band.isin(['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50']).sum()),
      'lifecycle_unknown_events':int(data.lifecycle_band.eq('UNKNOWN').sum()),
      'time_tolerance_seconds':1,
      'profile_timing_limitation':'Profiles attach to the original T0 offer event/order. Absent score generation/version timestamps prevent full proof that the current score snapshot was available at the event. No missing profile is filled using future outcomes.',
    }
    return data,quality


def count_arrays(d,eligible,selected):
    e=np.asarray(eligible,bool);s=np.asarray(selected,bool)
    y0=d.y_d0.to_numpy(float);y3=d.y_d3.to_numpy(float)
    k0=np.isfinite(y0);k3=np.isfinite(y3)
    mapped=d.profile_mapped.to_numpy(bool)
    valid=d.score_band.isin(['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50']).to_numpy(bool)
    return {
      'cohort_events':np.ones(len(d)),'cohort_d0_known':k0,'cohort_d0_noapply':y0==0,
      'eligible_events':e,'eligible_d0_known':e&k0,'eligible_d0_noapply':e&(y0==0),
      'selected_all_events':s,'selected_profile_mapped_events':s&mapped,
      'selected_valid_410_events':s&valid,'selected_d0_known_events':s&k0,
      'selected_d0_unknown_events':s&~k0,'selected_d0_noapply_events':s&(y0==0),
      'selected_d0_apply_events':s&(y0==1),'selected_d3_known_events':s&k3,
      'selected_d3_unknown_events':s&~k3,'selected_d3_noapply_events':s&(y3==0),
      'selected_d3_apply_events':s&(y3==1),'selected_d1_3_apply_events':s&(y0==0)&(y3==1),
    }


def derive(counts,lowp_all):
    pairs={
      'selection_coverage_all_scored':('selected_all_events','cohort_events'),
      'profile_mapping_coverage':('selected_profile_mapped_events','selected_all_events'),
      'selected_valid_410_share':('selected_valid_410_events','selected_all_events'),
      'd0_nonapply_precision':('selected_d0_noapply_events','selected_d0_known_events'),
      'd0_apply_proxy_leakage':('selected_d0_apply_events','selected_d0_known_events'),
      'd3_nonapply_precision_known_only':('selected_d3_noapply_events','selected_d3_known_events'),
      'd1_3_apply_share_known_d3_only':('selected_d1_3_apply_events','selected_d3_known_events'),
      'baseline_d0_nonapply_all_scored_known':('cohort_d0_noapply','cohort_d0_known'),
      'baseline_d0_nonapply_eligible_known':('eligible_d0_noapply','eligible_d0_known'),
      'd0_nonapply_capture_all_scored_known':('selected_d0_noapply_events','cohort_d0_noapply'),
    }
    result={k:ratio(counts[a],counts[b]) for k,(a,b) in pairs.items()}
    result['selection_share_of_all_lowp_candidates']=ratio(counts['selected_all_events'],lowp_all)
    result['d0_precision_lift_vs_global_baseline_pp']=100*(result['d0_nonapply_precision']-result['baseline_d0_nonapply_all_scored_known'])
    result['d0_precision_lift_vs_eligible_baseline_pp']=100*(result['d0_nonapply_precision']-result['baseline_d0_nonapply_eligible_known'])
    return result


def rule_specs(d):
    all_=np.ones(len(d),bool)
    score=d.score_band
    life=d.lifecycle_band
    high=score.eq('HIGH20').to_numpy(bool)
    mid=score.eq('MID30').to_numpy(bool)
    low=score.eq('LOW50').to_numpy(bool)
    six=life.isin(['6-10','11-20','21+']).to_numpy(bool)
    eleven=life.isin(['11-20','21+']).to_numpy(bool)
    rules=[('LOWP_ALL','低pD3全部候选',all_),('LOWP_HIGH20','低pD3且410高分前20%',high),
      ('LOWP_MID30','低pD3且410中间20%—50%',mid),('LOWP_HIGHMID50','低pD3且410高分前50%',high|mid),
      ('LOWP_LOW50','低pD3且410后50%',low),
      ('LOWP_INVALID_OR_MISSING_410','低pD3且410不可有效分档',~(high|mid|low)),
      ('LOWP_LIFE6PLUS','低pD3且本次结清为第6笔及以上',six),
      ('LOWP_LIFE11PLUS','低pD3且本次结清为第11笔及以上',eleven),
      ('LOWP_HIGHMID50_LIFE6PLUS','低pD3且410前50%且第6笔及以上', (high|mid)&six),
      ('LOWP_HIGH20_LIFE6PLUS','低pD3且410前20%且第6笔及以上',high&six),
      ('LOWP_HIGHMID50_LIFE11PLUS','低pD3且410前50%且第11笔及以上',(high|mid)&eleven),
      ('LOWP_HIGH20_LIFE11PLUS','低pD3且410前20%且第11笔及以上',high&eleven),
      ('LOWP_PROFILE_UNMAPPED','低pD3但画像无法可靠关联',~d.profile_mapped.to_numpy(bool))]
    rules +=[(f'LOWP_LIFE_{b}','低pD3且本次结清生命周期档'+b,life.eq(b).to_numpy(bool)) for b in LIFE_BANDS if b!='UNMAPPED']
    return rules


def analyze_rules(d):
    lowp=d.p_v4_d3.to_numpy(float)<.5
    codes,users=pd.factorize(d.user_id,sort=True)
    u=len(users);rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    boot=rng.multinomial(u,np.full(u,1/u),size=REPLICATES)
    lowp_user=np.bincount(codes,weights=lowp,minlength=u)
    boot_lowp=boot@lowp_user
    rows=[]
    for name,label,eligible in rule_specs(d):
        selected=eligible&lowp
        arrays=count_arrays(d,eligible,selected)
        counts={k:float(np.sum(v)) for k,v in arrays.items()}
        point=derive(counts,int(lowp.sum()))
        keys=list(arrays)
        user_matrix=np.column_stack([np.bincount(codes,weights=arrays[k],minlength=u) for k in keys])
        samples=boot@user_matrix
        bc={k:samples[:,i] for i,k in enumerate(keys)}
        bmetrics=derive(bc,boot_lowp)
        row={'rule':name,'description':label,**{k:int(v) for k,v in counts.items()},
             'cohort_users':u,'eligible_users':int(d.loc[eligible,'user_id'].nunique()),
             'selected_all_users':int(d.loc[selected,'user_id'].nunique()),
             'selected_d0_evaluable_users':int(d.loc[selected&d.y_d0.notna().to_numpy(),'user_id'].nunique()),
             'selected_d3_evaluable_users':int(d.loc[selected&d.y_d3.notna().to_numpy(),'user_id'].nunique()),
             **{k:float(v) for k,v in point.items()}}
        for k,v in bmetrics.items():
            f=np.asarray(v)[np.isfinite(v)]
            row[k+'_ci95_low']=float(np.quantile(f,.025)) if len(f) else None
            row[k+'_ci95_high']=float(np.quantile(f,.975)) if len(f) else None
            row[k+'_bootstrap_valid']=len(f)
        row['zero_observed_d0_error_warning']=bool(counts['selected_d0_known_events']>0 and counts['selected_d0_apply_events']==0)
        rows.append(row)
    return rows


def compositions(d):
    out=[]
    for name,mask in [('ALL_SCORED',np.ones(len(d),bool)),('LOWP_D3_LT_0_5',d.p_v4_d3.to_numpy()<.5)]:
        z=d.loc[mask];n=len(z);v=z.score_band.isin(['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50']).sum()
        for field,bands in [('score_band',SCORE_BANDS),('lifecycle_band',LIFE_BANDS)]:
            for b in bands:
                q=z[field].eq(b);valid=b in ['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50'] if field=='score_band' else False
                out.append({'population':name,'dimension':field,'band':b,'events':int(q.sum()),
                  'users':int(z.loc[q,'user_id'].nunique()),'population_events':n,'event_share':float(q.sum()/n) if n else None,
                  'valid_410_denominator':int(v) if field=='score_band' else None,
                  'share_among_valid_410_only':float(q.sum()/v) if valid and v else None})
    return out


def report_aliases(row,population='full8866'):
    """Stable short names for the shared report/Excel interface."""
    return {**row,'population':population,'name':row['description'],
      'all_candidates':row['selected_all_events'],'all_candidate_users':row['selected_all_users'],
      'known_d0':row['selected_d0_known_events'],'unknown_d0':row['selected_d0_unknown_events'],
      'no_d0':row['selected_d0_noapply_events'],'ppv_no_d0':row['d0_nonapply_precision'],
      'ci95_low':row['d0_nonapply_precision_ci95_low'],'ci95_high':row['d0_nonapply_precision_ci95_high'],
      'd0_apply':row['selected_d0_apply_events'],'d1_d3_apply':row['selected_d1_3_apply_events'],
      'd3_known':row['selected_d3_known_events'],'d3_unknown':row['selected_d3_unknown_events'],
      'd3_noapply':row['selected_d3_noapply_events'],'npv_d3':row['d3_nonapply_precision_known_only'],
      'all_candidate_coverage':row['selection_coverage_all_scored'],
      'share_of_model_lowp_candidates':row['selection_share_of_all_lowp_candidates']}


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser();parser.add_argument('--mapping',type=Path,default=ROOT/'validation_mapping.parquet');args=parser.parse_args()
    mapping=pd.read_parquet(args.mapping)
    source=OLD/'events_predictions.parquet';labels=OLD/'evaluation/labels_evaluated.parquet'
    pred=pd.read_parquet(source);d=pd.read_parquet(labels)
    if pred.event_id.duplicated().any() or d.event_id.duplicated().any():raise ValueError('Duplicate original validation event IDs')
    # Verify labels and probabilities used here are exactly the prior frozen data.
    a=pred.set_index('event_id').sort_index();b=d.set_index('event_id').sort_index()
    np.testing.assert_equal(a.index.to_numpy(),b.index.to_numpy())
    for c in ['user_id','as_of_time','p_v4_d0','p_v4_d3']:
        np.testing.assert_equal(a[c].to_numpy(),b[c].to_numpy())
    d=d[(d.offer_date>='2026-10-09')&(d.offer_date<='2026-10-21')].copy()
    assert len(d)==8866 and int((d.mature_d3&d.y_d3.notna()).sum())==8717
    assert not ((d.y_d0==1)&(d.y_d3==0)).any()
    d,quality=add_profiles(d,mapping)
    thresholds_path=ROOT/'thresholds.json'
    thresholds=json.loads(thresholds_path.read_text())
    v=d.score_band.isin(['HIGH20','MID30','LOW50'])
    score=pd.to_numeric(d.score_410,errors='coerce')
    expected=np.select([score.ge(thresholds['q80']),score.ge(thresholds['q50'])],['HIGH20','MID30'],default='LOW50')
    if (v & d.score_band.ne(expected)).any():raise ValueError('Supplied score_band disagrees with frozen full-cohort cutpoints')
    rows=[report_aliases(x) for x in analyze_rules(d)];comp=compositions(d)
    main_rows=[report_aliases(x,'main8717') for x in analyze_rules(d.loc[d.mature_d3&d.y_d3.notna()].copy())]
    allrow=next(x for x in rows if x['rule']=='LOWP_ALL')
    assert allrow['selected_all_events']==3701
    assert allrow['selected_d0_known_events']==3656
    assert allrow['selected_d0_noapply_events']==3143
    assert allrow['selected_d0_apply_events']==513
    assert allrow['selected_d3_unknown_events']==45
    assert allrow['selected_d1_3_apply_events']==655
    (ROOT/'prediction_profiles.csv').write_text(pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(index=False),encoding='utf-8')
    (ROOT/'prediction_profile_composition.csv').write_text(pd.DataFrame(comp).to_csv(index=False),encoding='utf-8')
    (ROOT/'prediction_profile_mapping_quality.csv').write_text(pd.DataFrame([quality]).to_csv(index=False),encoding='utf-8')
    keep=['event_id','user_id','as_of_time','p_v4_d0','p_v4_d3','y_d0','y_d3','mature_d3','profile_key_status','score_410','score_status','score_band','settled_loan_seq','lifecycle_band']
    d[keep].to_parquet(ROOT/'prediction_profiles_event_audit.parquet',index=False)
    result={
      'contract':{
        'model':'Frozen T0 timing V4; no retraining or recalibration',
        'population':'All 8,866 scored strict T0 events on October9-21, including149 events with unknown outcomes; 8,717 D3 mature and label-known events form the principal evaluable cohort.',
        'selection':'Original unnormalized p_v4_d3<0.5; all3,701 candidates retained, including45 unknown outcomes. No outcome is required for selection.',
        'horizon':'D0 is after offer until next local midnight. D3 is D0-D3 through event_date+4days exclusive, not rolling72hours.',
        'cutoff':'2026-10-25 00:00:00 America/Belize exclusive',
        'd0_precision_denominator':'Selected events with known D0 outcome; unknown not imputed. Complete candidate counts/coverage are shown separately.',
        'd3_precision_and_delay_denominator':'Selected events with known D3 outcome, not the entire selected candidate count. D1-D3 is yD0=0,yD3=1.',
        'profile_source':'Risk band and settled-loan sequence from original passed T0 order mapping; score410 cutpoints are fixed from the full January-October population before outcomes. No per-stage or per-model-subset quantile refitting.',
        'life_sequence_definition':'Use supplied settled_loan_seq of the clean-settlement order triggering this T0. It is not a current/future lifetime total and not inferred from subsequent borrowing.',
        'rules_status':'All joint eligibility rules are candidate descriptions. No optimal threshold or production rollout is chosen from this replay.',
        'missing_profile_policy':'Unmatched/time-mismatched profiles remain UNMAPPED. Missing/out-of-range/conflicted410 is never automatically treated as favorable. UNKNOWN lifecycle not forced to another band.',
        'bootstrap':f'{REPLICATES} user-cluster percentile resamples, all events for the same user together, fixed eligibility rules and source mapping. CIs do not resolve missing/profile-time/source bias. Sparse zero-failure groups can have degenerate percentile intervals.',
        'seed':SEED,
        'causal_limit':'Observed non-application under historical policies is not a measure of incentive responsiveness or incremental profit. High risk score/lifecycle retention does not itself demonstrate safe or profitable extra lending.',
      },
      'source_files':{str(source):sha(source),str(labels):sha(labels),str(args.mapping):sha(args.mapping),str(thresholds_path):sha(thresholds_path)},
      'score_band_frozen_metadata':thresholds,
      'mapping_quality':quality,'match_quality':quality,'rules':rows,'compositions':comp,
      'rules_main_evaluable':main_rows,
      'group_composition':[{**x,
        'population':{'ALL_SCORED':'full8866','LOWP_D3_LT_0_5':'selected3701'}[x['population']],
        'dimension':{'score_band':'risk','lifecycle_band':'life'}[x['dimension']],
        'share':x['event_share']} for x in comp],
      'crosscheck_original_targeting':{
        'all_scored':len(d),'evaluable_d3':int(d.y_d3.notna().sum()),
        'all_candidates':allrow['selected_all_events'],'candidate_d0_known':allrow['selected_d0_known_events'],
        'candidate_d0_noapply':allrow['selected_d0_noapply_events'],'candidate_d0_apply':allrow['selected_d0_apply_events'],
        'candidate_d3_unknown':allrow['selected_d3_unknown_events'],'passed':True,
      },
    }
    result['meta']=result['contract']
    result['meta']['q50']=thresholds['q50']
    result['meta']['q80']=thresholds['q80']
    result['meta']['valid_cutpoint_population_events']=thresholds['valid_n']
    keyed={(x['population'],x['dimension'],x['band']):x for x in comp}
    contrast=[]
    for dimension,bands in [('score_band',SCORE_BANDS),('lifecycle_band',LIFE_BANDS)]:
        for band in bands:
            full=keyed['ALL_SCORED',dimension,band]
            selected=keyed['LOWP_D3_LT_0_5',dimension,band]
            contrast.append({'dimension':{'score_band':'risk','lifecycle_band':'life'}[dimension],
              'band':band,'all_scored_events':full['events'],'lowp_candidate_events':selected['events'],
              'all_scored_share':full['event_share'],'lowp_candidate_share':selected['event_share'],
              'lowp_minus_full_share_pp':100*(selected['event_share']-full['event_share'])})
    result['composition_contrast']=contrast
    json_write(ROOT/'prediction_profiles.json',result)
    print(json.dumps(safe({'output':str(ROOT/'prediction_profiles.json'),'mapping_quality':quality,'rules':len(rows),'all_candidates':allrow['selected_all_events']}),ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__=='__main__':main()
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_risk410_lifecycle_strategy_20261025/sql/strict_t0_risk410_lifecycle_global.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：offers_fb2f12830c34defe
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
WITH params AS (
    SELECT DATE '2026-01-01' AS base_start_date,
           DATE '2026-01-01' AS output_start_date,
           DATE '2026-11-01' AS output_end_date,
           TIMESTAMP '2026-10-25 09:55:21.800090' AS data_cutoff_ts
), raw_pass_offer AS (
    SELECT v.user_id, v.serial_id, v.vir_date::date AS vir_date,
           v.vir_unix,
           TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize' AS vir_time
    FROM wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v CROSS JOIN params p
    WHERE v.is_pass1 = 1 AND v.loan_type_code = 2 AND v.vir_unix > 0
      AND v.vir_date >= p.base_start_date - 1
      AND v.vir_date < p.data_cutoff_ts::date + 1
      AND TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize' < p.data_cutoff_ts
), qualified_offer AS (
    SELECT v.* FROM raw_pass_offer v
    WHERE EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM wangchuanliang.side_recycle_type_copy r
        WHERE r.serial_id = v.serial_id AND r.recycle_type = 3
    )
), serial_latest AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT ON (user_id, serial_id) * FROM qualified_offer
    ORDER BY user_id, serial_id, vir_unix DESC
), lagged AS (
    SELECT v.*, LAG(vir_time) OVER (
        PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time, serial_id
    ) AS previous_time
    FROM serial_latest v
), batched AS (
    SELECT v.*, SUM(CASE WHEN previous_time IS NULL
                              OR vir_time - previous_time > INTERVAL '20 seconds'
                        THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) OVER (
        PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time, serial_id ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING
    ) AS batch_id
    FROM lagged v
), batch_ranked AS (
    SELECT v.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
        PARTITION BY user_id, batch_id ORDER BY vir_time DESC, serial_id DESC
    ) AS batch_rn
    FROM batched v
), dedup_offer AS (
    SELECT v.* FROM batch_ranked v CROSS JOIN params p
    WHERE batch_rn = 1 AND vir_date >= p.base_start_date
), candidate_users AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT user_id FROM dedup_offer
), funded_orders AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT ON (o.user_id, o.serial_id)
           o.user_id, o.serial_id, o.apply_time, o.remit_time,
           o.remit_amt, o.is_remit, o.repaid_time, o.repaid_date,
           o.is_repaid, o.loan_status_code
    FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy o
    JOIN candidate_users u ON u.user_id = o.user_id
    WHERE o.is_remit = 1 AND o.remit_amt > 0
    ORDER BY o.user_id, o.serial_id, o.apply_time ASC NULLS LAST
), clean_settlement AS (
    SELECT o.* FROM funded_orders o CROSS JOIN params p
    WHERE (o.is_repaid = 1 OR o.loan_status_code = 7)
      AND o.repaid_time > TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01'
      AND o.repaid_date >= p.base_start_date
      AND o.repaid_time < p.data_cutoff_ts
      AND NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM funded_orders x
          WHERE x.user_id = o.user_id AND x.serial_id <> o.serial_id
            AND x.remit_time <= o.repaid_time
            AND (x.repaid_time IS NULL
                 OR x.repaid_time <= TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01'
                 OR x.repaid_time > o.repaid_time)
      )
), settlement_matched AS (
    SELECT e.*, s.serial_id AS settled_serial_id, s.repaid_time,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
               PARTITION BY e.user_id, e.serial_id
               ORDER BY s.repaid_time DESC, s.serial_id DESC
           ) AS settlement_rn
    FROM dedup_offer e JOIN clean_settlement s
      ON s.user_id = e.user_id AND s.repaid_date = e.vir_date
     AND s.repaid_time <= e.vir_time
), strict_t0_all AS (
    SELECT e.*, LEAD(vir_time) OVER (
        PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time, serial_id
    ) AS next_t0_time
    FROM settlement_matched e WHERE settlement_rn = 1
), cohort AS (
    SELECT e.*, p.data_cutoff_ts FROM strict_t0_all e CROSS JOIN params p
    WHERE e.vir_date >= p.output_start_date AND e.vir_date < p.output_end_date
), raw_applications AS (
    SELECT o.user_id,o.serial_id,o.apply_time,o.apply_date,o.apply_amt,
           o.is_remit,o.remit_time,o.remit_amt,o.repaid_amt,o.due_date,o.is_due,
           o.loan_status_code,o.loan_day,o.total_term,
           COUNT(*) OVER(PARTITION BY o.user_id,o.serial_id) AS source_order_rows
    FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy o
    JOIN candidate_users u ON u.user_id=o.user_id CROSS JOIN params p
    WHERE o.apply_date>=p.base_start_date-1
      AND o.apply_date<p.data_cutoff_ts::date+1
      AND o.apply_time>=p.base_start_date::timestamp AND o.apply_time<p.data_cutoff_ts
), applications AS (
    SELECT DISTINCT ON (user_id,serial_id) * FROM raw_applications
    ORDER BY user_id,serial_id,apply_time
), application_ranked AS (
    SELECT e.user_id,e.serial_id AS credit_serial_id,e.vir_date,e.vir_time,
           e.settled_serial_id,e.repaid_time AS clean_settle_time,
           e.next_t0_time,e.data_cutoff_ts,
           o.serial_id AS apply_serial_id,o.apply_time,o.apply_amt,o.is_remit,
           o.remit_time,o.remit_amt,o.repaid_amt,o.due_date,o.is_due,
           o.loan_status_code,o.loan_day,o.total_term,o.source_order_rows,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY e.user_id,e.serial_id
                              ORDER BY o.apply_time,o.serial_id) AS apply_rn
    FROM cohort e LEFT JOIN applications o
      ON o.user_id=e.user_id AND o.apply_time>=e.vir_time
     AND (e.next_t0_time IS NULL OR o.apply_time<e.next_t0_time)
),
 r410_events AS (
