 SELECT * FROM application_ranked WHERE apply_rn=1 AND vir_date<data_cutoff_ts::date
 ), r410_fundseq AS (
 SELECT o.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY remit_time,serial_id) AS settled_loan_seq,
   SUM(CASE WHEN remit_time IS NULL OR remit_time<=TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
      OVER(PARTITION BY user_id) AS history_bad_remit_clock_n
 FROM funded_orders o
 ), r410_model AS (
 SELECT m.user_id,m.serial_id,COUNT(*) AS score_source_rows,
   COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre BETWEEN 1 AND 3000 THEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre END) AS distinct_valid_scores,
   CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre BETWEEN 1 AND 3000 THEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre END)=1
     THEN MIN(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre BETWEEN 1 AND 3000 THEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre END) END AS score_410,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_null_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre BETWEEN 1 AND 3000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_valid_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre=-9999999 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_placeholder_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre<=0 AND m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre<>-9999999 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_nonpositive_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre>3000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_above3000_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre IS NOT NULL AND NOT(m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre BETWEEN 1 AND 3000)
        AND m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre<>-9999999 AND NOT(m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre<=0) AND NOT(m.kabybmerge410fpd7cre>3000)
       THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_other_invalid_rows
 FROM wangchuanliang.model_result_copy m
 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r410_events e WHERE e.user_id=m.user_id AND e.credit_serial_id=m.serial_id)
 GROUP BY m.user_id,m.serial_id
 ), r410_profiles_unbinned AS (
 SELECT e.*,m.score_410,m.score_source_rows,m.distinct_valid_scores,
   COALESCE(m.score_null_rows,0) AS score_null_rows,COALESCE(m.score_valid_rows,0) AS score_valid_rows,
   COALESCE(m.score_placeholder_rows,0) AS score_placeholder_rows,
   COALESCE(m.score_nonpositive_rows,0) AS score_nonpositive_rows,
   COALESCE(m.score_above3000_rows,0) AS score_above3000_rows,
   COALESCE(m.score_other_invalid_rows,0) AS score_other_invalid_rows,
   CASE WHEN m.serial_id IS NULL THEN 'NO_RECORD'
        WHEN m.distinct_valid_scores>1 THEN 'CONFLICT'
        WHEN m.distinct_valid_scores=1 THEN 'VALID'
        WHEN m.score_null_rows=m.score_source_rows THEN 'NULL_SCORE' ELSE 'INVALID' END AS score_status,
   CASE WHEN s.history_bad_remit_clock_n=0 AND s.remit_time<=e.clean_settle_time
             AND s.remit_time<=e.vir_time THEN s.settled_loan_seq END AS settled_loan_seq,
   CASE WHEN s.serial_id IS NULL THEN 'SETTLED_ORDER_UNMATCHED'
        WHEN s.history_bad_remit_clock_n>0 THEN 'HISTORY_REMIT_CLOCK_MISSING'
        WHEN s.remit_time>e.clean_settle_time OR s.remit_time>e.vir_time THEN 'INVALID_CHRONOLOGY'
        ELSE 'VALID' END AS sequence_status,
   s.history_bad_remit_clock_n,
   e.apply_time::date-e.vir_date AS apply_day,
   CASE WHEN e.is_remit=1 AND e.remit_amt>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS funded,
   e.data_cutoff_ts::date-e.due_date AS recovery_age_days
 FROM r410_events e LEFT JOIN r410_model m
   ON m.user_id=e.user_id AND m.serial_id=e.credit_serial_id
 LEFT JOIN r410_fundseq s ON s.user_id=e.user_id AND s.serial_id=e.settled_serial_id
 ), r410_ordered_scores AS (
 SELECT score_410,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY score_410) AS score_rn,COUNT(*) OVER() AS valid_n
 FROM r410_profiles_unbinned WHERE score_status='VALID'
 ), r410_thresholds AS (
 SELECT MAX(valid_n) AS valid_n,
   MIN(CASE WHEN score_rn>=CEIL(valid_n*0.5) THEN score_410 END) AS q50,
   MIN(CASE WHEN score_rn>=CEIL(valid_n*0.8) THEN score_410 END) AS q80
 FROM r410_ordered_scores
 ), r410_profiles AS (
 SELECT e.*,
   CASE WHEN score_status<>'VALID' THEN score_status
        WHEN score_410>=t.q80 THEN 'HIGH20'
        WHEN score_410>=t.q50 THEN 'MID30' ELSE 'LOW50' END AS score_band,
   CASE WHEN settled_loan_seq IS NULL THEN 'UNKNOWN'
        WHEN settled_loan_seq BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN settled_loan_seq::text
        WHEN settled_loan_seq BETWEEN 6 AND 10 THEN '6-10'
        WHEN settled_loan_seq BETWEEN 11 AND 20 THEN '11-20' ELSE '21+' END AS lifecycle_band
 FROM r410_profiles_unbinned e CROSS JOIN r410_thresholds t
 ), r410_bucket_defs AS (
 SELECT 'D0'::text AS bucket UNION ALL SELECT 'D1' UNION ALL SELECT 'D2'
 UNION ALL SELECT 'D3' UNION ALL SELECT 'D4+' UNION ALL SELECT 'D4-D15'
 UNION ALL SELECT 'D16+' UNION ALL SELECT 'D1-D3' UNION ALL SELECT 'D1+'
 UNION ALL SELECT 'ALL' UNION ALL SELECT 'no_apply'
 ), r410_staged AS (
 SELECT e.*,b.bucket
 FROM r410_profiles e CROSS JOIN r410_bucket_defs b
 WHERE b.bucket='ALL' OR (b.bucket='no_apply' AND apply_serial_id IS NULL)
    OR (b.bucket='D0' AND apply_day=0) OR (b.bucket='D1' AND apply_day=1)
    OR (b.bucket='D2' AND apply_day=2) OR (b.bucket='D3' AND apply_day=3)
    OR (b.bucket='D4+' AND apply_day>=4) OR (b.bucket='D4-D15' AND apply_day BETWEEN 4 AND 15)
    OR (b.bucket='D16+' AND apply_day>=16) OR (b.bucket='D1-D3' AND apply_day BETWEEN 1 AND 3)
    OR (b.bucket='D1+' AND apply_day>=1)
 ), r410_grained AS (
 SELECT e.*,'event'::text AS grain FROM r410_staged e
 ), r410_scopes AS (
 SELECT 'main'::text AS scope,'mature_complete_days'::text AS scenario
 UNION ALL SELECT 'jan_sep','mature_complete_days'
 UNION ALL SELECT 'recovery30','mature_30d'
 UNION ALL SELECT 'all_funded','all_funded'
 ), r410_scoped AS (
 SELECT e.*,s.scope,s.scenario,
   CASE WHEN funded=1 AND ((s.scenario='all_funded')
     OR (s.scenario='mature_complete_days' AND due_date>=DATE '2000-01-01' AND due_date<data_cutoff_ts::date)
     OR (s.scenario='mature_30d' AND due_date>=DATE '2000-01-01' AND recovery_age_days>=30)) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS eligible
 FROM r410_grained e CROSS JOIN r410_scopes s
 WHERE s.scope<>'jan_sep' OR vir_date<DATE '2026-10-01'
 ), r410_periods AS (
 SELECT e.*,'ALL'::text AS period FROM r410_scoped e
 UNION ALL SELECT e.*,TO_CHAR(vir_date,'YYYY-MM') FROM r410_scoped e WHERE scope='main'
 ), r410_cells AS (
 SELECT scope,grain,period,scenario,bucket,
   CASE WHEN GROUPING(score_band)=0 AND GROUPING(lifecycle_band)=0 THEN 'joint'
        WHEN GROUPING(score_band)=0 THEN 'risk' WHEN GROUPING(lifecycle_band)=0 THEN 'lifecycle'
        ELSE 'overall' END AS level,
   CASE WHEN GROUPING(score_band)=1 THEN 'ALL' ELSE score_band END AS score_band,
   CASE WHEN GROUPING(lifecycle_band)=1 THEN 'ALL' ELSE lifecycle_band END AS lifecycle_band,
   COUNT(*) AS event_count,COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS users,COUNT(apply_serial_id) AS first_apply_orders,
   SUM(funded) AS funded_orders,SUM(eligible) AS mature_orders,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND repaid_amt IS NOT NULL THEN remit_amt ELSE 0 END) AS principal,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND repaid_amt IS NOT NULL THEN repaid_amt ELSE 0 END) AS repaid,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND repaid_amt IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_repaid_orders,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND repaid_amt IS NULL THEN remit_amt ELSE 0 END) AS missing_repaid_principal,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND loan_status_code=8 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS state8_orders,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='VALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_valid_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='CONFLICT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_conflict_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='INVALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_invalid_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='NO_RECORD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_no_record_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='NULL_SCORE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_null_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN sequence_status<>'VALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS lifetime_unknown_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND loan_day IS NOT NULL THEN loan_day ELSE 0 END) AS loan_day_sum,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND loan_day IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS loan_day_n,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND recovery_age_days IS NOT NULL THEN recovery_age_days ELSE 0 END) AS recovery_age_sum,
   SUM(CASE WHEN eligible=1 AND recovery_age_days IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS recovery_age_n
 FROM r410_periods
 GROUP BY GROUPING SETS (
  (scope,grain,period,scenario,bucket,score_band,lifecycle_band),
  (scope,grain,period,scenario,bucket,score_band),
  (scope,grain,period,scenario,bucket,lifecycle_band),
  (scope,grain,period,scenario,bucket))
 ), r410_exact_lifetime AS (
 SELECT grain,bucket,settled_loan_seq,COUNT(*) AS event_count,COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS users,
   SUM(funded) AS funded_orders,
   SUM(CASE WHEN funded=1 AND due_date>=DATE '2000-01-01' AND due_date<data_cutoff_ts::date THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS mature_orders
 FROM r410_grained GROUP BY grain,bucket,settled_loan_seq
 ), r410_quality AS (
 SELECT COUNT(*) AS events,COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS users,
   COUNT(*)-COUNT(DISTINCT user_id::text||':'||credit_serial_id::text) AS duplicate_events,
   COUNT(apply_serial_id)-COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN apply_serial_id IS NOT NULL THEN user_id::text||':'||apply_serial_id::text END) AS duplicate_first_order_assignments,
   SUM(CASE WHEN source_order_rows>1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS duplicate_financial_source_orders,
   SUM(CASE WHEN clean_settle_time>vir_time OR vir_time::date<>vir_date OR apply_time<vir_time OR apply_time>=next_t0_time OR apply_time>=data_cutoff_ts THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS invalid_time_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='VALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_valid_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='CONFLICT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_conflict_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='INVALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_invalid_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='NO_RECORD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_no_record_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='NULL_SCORE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS score_null_events,
   SUM(COALESCE(score_source_rows,0)) AS score_source_rows,
   SUM(score_null_rows) AS score_null_rows,SUM(score_valid_rows) AS score_valid_rows,
   SUM(score_placeholder_rows) AS score_placeholder_rows,SUM(score_nonpositive_rows) AS score_nonpositive_rows,
   SUM(score_above3000_rows) AS score_above3000_rows,SUM(score_other_invalid_rows) AS score_other_invalid_rows,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_source_rows>1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS model_duplicate_row_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN score_status='VALID' AND (score_placeholder_rows+score_nonpositive_rows+score_above3000_rows+score_other_invalid_rows)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS valid_with_invalid_companion_events,
   SUM(CASE WHEN sequence_status<>'VALID' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS lifetime_unknown_events,
   MIN(settled_loan_seq) AS min_settled_loan_seq,MAX(settled_loan_seq) AS max_settled_loan_seq,
   MIN(vir_date) AS first_event_date,MAX(vir_date) AS last_event_date,
   MAX(data_cutoff_ts) AS observation_cutoff
 FROM r410_profiles
 ), r410_validation_mapping AS (
 SELECT credit_serial_id AS event_id,credit_serial_id,user_id,vir_time,TO_CHAR(vir_date,'YYYY-MM') AS month,
   score_410,score_status,score_band,NULL::timestamp AS score_time,
   settled_serial_id,settled_loan_seq,lifecycle_band,sequence_status,score_source_rows,distinct_valid_scores
 FROM r410_profiles WHERE credit_serial_id IN (SELECT credit_serial_id FROM validation_target_offers WHERE batch_key = 'offers_fb2f12830c34defe')
 )
 SELECT 'cell'::text AS record_type,row_to_json(x)::text AS payload FROM r410_cells x
 UNION ALL SELECT 'exact_lifecycle',row_to_json(x)::text FROM r410_exact_lifetime x
 UNION ALL SELECT 'quality',row_to_json(x)::text FROM r410_quality x
 UNION ALL SELECT 'thresholds',row_to_json(x)::text FROM r410_thresholds x
 UNION ALL SELECT 'validation_mapping',row_to_json(x)::text FROM r410_validation_mapping x

) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_offers WHERE batch_key = 'offers_fb2f12830c34defe');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/all_users_daily_20261001_20261025.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_a3f8e8645b0e0307
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_a3f8e8645b0e0307')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2026-10-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2026-10-25'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_a3f8e8645b0e0307');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/credits.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY v.user_id,v.vir_unix,v.serial_id) AS export_id,
 v.user_id::bigint,v.serial_id::bigint,
 (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize') AS credit_time,
 v.is_pass1,v.max_credit_apply_amt::float8 AS credit_amt,v.risk_product
FROM wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v
WHERE v.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e')
AND v.vir_unix>0 AND v.vir_unix<%(cutoff_unix)s
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/event_behavior_202610_block00.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：events_699a8f3949ef9bb8
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
WITH target_events(event_id,user_id,as_of_time) AS (SELECT event_id,user_id,as_of_time FROM validation_target_events WHERE batch_key = 'events_699a8f3949ef9bb8')
SELECT t.event_id,
COUNT(CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days' THEN e.user_id END)::float8 AS beh_events_7d,
COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days' THEN e.create_date END)::float8 AS beh_active_days_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '1 day' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_events_1d,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '3 days' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_events_3d,
SUM(CASE WHEN (e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') AND (e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_newpage_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN (e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') AND (e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_amount_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN (e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_plan_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN (e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') AND (e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_term_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN (e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_confirm_7d,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days' THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM(t.as_of_time AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS beh_duration_7d,
EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM(t.as_of_time-MAX(CASE WHEN e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days' THEN e.create_time END)))/86400.0 AS days_since_active,
COUNT(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date THEN e.user_id END)::float8 AS prefix_events,
MAX(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date THEN e.create_time END) AS prefix_last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select')) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND (lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM(t.as_of_time AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM(t.as_of_time AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS prefix_apply_duration
FROM target_events t LEFT JOIN wangchuanliang.event_track_record e ON e.user_id=t.user_id
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2026-09-24' AND e.create_date<DATE '2026-11-01'
AND e.create_time<t.as_of_time AND ((e.create_time>=t.as_of_time-INTERVAL '7 days') OR (e.create_date=t.as_of_time::date))
GROUP BY t.event_id,t.as_of_time
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_events WHERE batch_key = 'events_699a8f3949ef9bb8');

t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/event_features_october.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
WITH
tmp_t0ml_params AS (
SELECT DATE '2026-10-01' AS start_date,DATE '2026-10-25' AS end_date,TIMESTAMP '2026-10-25 00:00:00' AS data_cutoff_ts
),
tmp_t0ml_offer AS (
SELECT DISTINCT ON (v.user_id,v.serial_id)
       v.user_id,v.serial_id,v.vir_date::date AS vir_date,
       (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize') AS vir_time,
       v.max_credit_apply_amt::float8 AS current_credit_amt,
       v.total_term::float8 AS current_total_term,
       v.risk_product,v.vir_version
FROM wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v CROSS JOIN tmp_t0ml_params p
WHERE v.is_pass1=1 AND v.loan_type_code=2
  AND v.vir_date>=p.start_date-1 AND v.vir_date<p.data_cutoff_ts::date+1
  AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM wangchuanliang.side_recycle_type_copy r
              WHERE r.serial_id=v.serial_id AND r.recycle_type=3)
ORDER BY v.user_id,v.serial_id,v.vir_unix DESC
),
tmp_t0ml_dedup AS (
WITH lagged AS (
 SELECT v.*,LAG(vir_time) OVER(PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time,serial_id) AS previous_time
 FROM tmp_t0ml_offer v
), batched AS (
 SELECT l.*,SUM(CASE WHEN previous_time IS NULL OR vir_time-previous_time>INTERVAL '20 seconds'
                    THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)
 OVER(PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time,serial_id ROWS UNBOUNDED PRECEDING) AS batch_id
 FROM lagged l
), ranked AS (
 SELECT b.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY user_id,batch_id ORDER BY vir_time DESC,serial_id DESC) AS rn
 FROM batched b
)
SELECT r.* FROM ranked r CROSS JOIN tmp_t0ml_params p
WHERE rn=1 AND vir_date>=p.start_date AND vir_time<p.data_cutoff_ts
),
tmp_t0ml_orders AS (
SELECT DISTINCT ON(o.user_id,o.serial_id)
 o.user_id,o.serial_id,o.apply_time,o.apply_date,o.apply_amt::float8,
 o.remit_time,o.remit_date,o.remit_amt::float8,o.is_remit,
 o.repaid_time,o.repaid_date,o.is_repaid,o.loan_status_code,
 o.due_date,o.total_term::float8,o.loan_day::float8,
 o.pre_amt::float8,o.post_amt::float8,o.remit_succ_rnb,
 o.reg_date,o.product_no,o.loan_type,o.repaid_amt::float8
FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy o
JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT user_id FROM tmp_t0ml_dedup) u ON u.user_id=o.user_id
ORDER BY o.user_id,o.serial_id,o.apply_time ASC
),
tmp_t0ml_settlement AS (
SELECT o.* FROM tmp_t0ml_orders o CROSS JOIN tmp_t0ml_params p
WHERE o.is_remit=1 AND o.remit_amt>0
 AND (o.is_repaid=1 OR o.loan_status_code=7)
 AND o.repaid_time> TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01'
 AND o.repaid_date>=p.start_date AND o.repaid_time<p.data_cutoff_ts
 AND NOT EXISTS (
  SELECT 1 FROM tmp_t0ml_orders x
  WHERE x.user_id=o.user_id AND x.serial_id<>o.serial_id
    AND x.is_remit=1 AND x.remit_amt>0 AND x.remit_time<=o.repaid_time
    AND (x.repaid_time IS NULL OR x.repaid_time<=TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01'
         OR x.repaid_time>o.repaid_time)
 )
),
tmp_t0ml_events_all AS (
WITH matched AS (
 SELECT e.*,s.serial_id AS settled_serial_id,s.repaid_time,
  ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY e.user_id,e.serial_id ORDER BY s.repaid_time DESC,s.serial_id DESC) AS srn
 FROM tmp_t0ml_dedup e JOIN tmp_t0ml_settlement s
 ON s.user_id=e.user_id AND s.repaid_date=e.vir_date AND s.repaid_time<=e.vir_time
)
SELECT m.*,LEAD(vir_time) OVER(PARTITION BY user_id ORDER BY vir_time,serial_id) AS next_t0_time
FROM matched m WHERE srn=1
),
tmp_t0ml_events AS (
SELECT e.*,MIN(o.apply_time) AS first_apply_time,
 LEAST(COALESCE(e.next_t0_time,p.data_cutoff_ts),p.data_cutoff_ts,
       e.vir_date+INTERVAL '16 days') AS followup_end,
 p.data_cutoff_ts
FROM tmp_t0ml_events_all e CROSS JOIN tmp_t0ml_params p
LEFT JOIN tmp_t0ml_orders o ON o.user_id=e.user_id
 AND o.apply_time>=e.vir_time AND o.apply_time<p.data_cutoff_ts
 AND o.apply_time<e.vir_date+INTERVAL '16 days'
 AND (e.next_t0_time IS NULL OR o.apply_time<e.next_t0_time)
WHERE e.vir_date<p.end_date
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.serial_id,e.vir_date,e.vir_time,e.current_credit_amt,
 e.current_total_term,e.risk_product,e.vir_version,e.previous_time,e.batch_id,
 e.rn,e.settled_serial_id,e.repaid_time,e.srn,e.next_t0_time,p.data_cutoff_ts
),
tmp_t0ml_order_credit AS (
SELECT DISTINCT ON (o.user_id,o.serial_id)
 o.user_id,o.serial_id,v.max_credit_apply_amt::float8 AS credit_amt,
 (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize') AS credit_time
FROM tmp_t0ml_orders o JOIN wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v
 ON v.user_id=o.user_id AND v.serial_id=o.serial_id AND v.is_pass1=1
 AND (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize')<=o.apply_time
ORDER BY o.user_id,o.serial_id,v.vir_unix DESC
),
tmp_t0ml_order_history AS (
WITH ranked AS (
 SELECT e.serial_id AS event_id,o.*,
  v.credit_amt,v.credit_time,
  ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY e.serial_id ORDER BY o.apply_time DESC,o.serial_id DESC) AS arn,
  ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY e.serial_id
    ORDER BY CASE WHEN o.is_remit=1 AND o.remit_time<=e.vir_time THEN o.remit_time END DESC NULLS LAST,
             o.serial_id DESC) AS rrn,
  CASE WHEN o.is_remit=1 AND o.remit_amt>0 AND o.remit_time<=e.vir_time THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS funded,
  CASE WHEN o.repaid_time>TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01' AND o.repaid_time<=e.vir_time THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS settled
 FROM tmp_t0ml_events e JOIN tmp_t0ml_orders o ON o.user_id=e.user_id AND o.apply_time<e.vir_time
 LEFT JOIN tmp_t0ml_order_credit v ON v.user_id=o.user_id AND v.serial_id=o.serial_id
), calculated AS (
 SELECT r.*,
  CASE WHEN apply_amt>0 AND credit_amt>0 THEN apply_amt/credit_amt END AS utilization,
  CASE WHEN credit_time IS NOT NULL THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (apply_time-credit_time))/86400.0 END AS credit_apply_gap_days
 FROM ranked r
)
SELECT event_id,COUNT(*)::float8 AS historical_apply_count,
 SUM(funded)::float8 AS successful_loan_count,
 SUM(CASE WHEN funded=1 THEN remit_amt ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS total_remit_amt,
 SUM(CASE WHEN settled=1 AND repaid_date<due_date THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS early_settle_count,
 SUM(CASE WHEN settled=1 AND repaid_date>due_date THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS late_settle_count,
 MAX(CASE WHEN settled=1 THEN GREATEST(repaid_date-due_date,0) END)::float8 AS max_past_late_days,
 MIN(reg_date) AS registration_date,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=1 THEN apply_amt END) AS apply_amt_l1,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=2 THEN apply_amt END) AS apply_amt_l2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=3 THEN apply_amt END) AS apply_amt_l3,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=1 THEN utilization END) AS apply_util_l1,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=2 THEN utilization END) AS apply_util_l2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=3 THEN utilization END) AS apply_util_l3,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=1 THEN credit_apply_gap_days END) AS apply_gap_l1,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=2 THEN credit_apply_gap_days END) AS apply_gap_l2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN arn=3 THEN credit_apply_gap_days END) AS apply_gap_l3,
 MAX(CASE WHEN funded=1 AND rrn=1 THEN remit_amt END) AS remit_amt_l1,
 MAX(CASE WHEN funded=1 AND rrn=2 THEN remit_amt END) AS remit_amt_l2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN funded=1 AND rrn=3 THEN remit_amt END) AS remit_amt_l3,
 MAX(apply_time) AS last_apply_time,
 MAX(CASE WHEN funded=1 THEN remit_time END) AS last_remit_time
FROM calculated GROUP BY event_id
),
tmp_t0ml_credit_history AS (
WITH eligible AS (
 SELECT e.serial_id AS event_id,v.serial_id,v.vir_unix,v.is_pass1,
 v.max_credit_apply_amt::float8 AS credit_amt,
 ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY e.serial_id,v.serial_id ORDER BY v.vir_unix DESC) AS srn
 FROM tmp_t0ml_events e JOIN wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v
 ON v.user_id=e.user_id
 AND (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize')<=e.vir_time
), ranked AS (
 SELECT a.*,ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY event_id
   ORDER BY CASE WHEN is_pass1=1 THEN vir_unix END DESC NULLS LAST,serial_id DESC) AS prn
 FROM eligible a WHERE srn=1
)
SELECT event_id,COUNT(*)::float8 AS credit_event_count,
 SUM(CASE WHEN is_pass1=1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS credit_pass_count,
 MAX(CASE WHEN is_pass1=1 AND prn=1 THEN credit_amt END) AS credit_amt_l1,
 MAX(CASE WHEN is_pass1=1 AND prn=2 THEN credit_amt END) AS credit_amt_l2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN is_pass1=1 AND prn=3 THEN credit_amt END) AS credit_amt_l3
FROM ranked GROUP BY event_id
),
tmp_t0ml_features AS (
SELECT e.serial_id AS event_id,e.user_id,e.serial_id AS credit_serial_id,e.settled_serial_id,
 e.vir_date,e.vir_time AS as_of_time,e.first_apply_time,e.followup_end,e.next_t0_time,e.data_cutoff_ts,
 e.current_credit_amt,e.current_total_term,e.risk_product,e.vir_version,
 c.credit_amt AS previous_credit_amt,
 p.apply_amt AS previous_apply_amt,p.remit_amt AS previous_remit_amt,
 p.total_term AS previous_total_term,p.loan_day AS previous_loan_day,
 CASE WHEN p.remit_amt>0 THEN (p.pre_amt+p.post_amt)/p.remit_amt END AS previous_fee_rate,
 CASE WHEN p.loan_day>0 AND p.remit_amt>0 THEN (p.pre_amt+p.post_amt)/p.remit_amt/p.loan_day END AS previous_daily_rate,
 (p.repaid_date-p.due_date)::float8 AS previous_settlement_delay,
 p.product_no AS previous_product_no,
 p.remit_succ_rnb AS original_successful_loan_rnb,
 CASE WHEN p.apply_amt>0 AND c.credit_amt>0 THEN p.apply_amt/c.credit_amt END AS previous_utilization,
 NULLIF(m.kabybxgboost405apd7crecyc,-9999999) AS risk_score_405,
 NULLIF(m.kabybxgboost363dpd7cre,-9999999) AS risk_score_363,
 h.historical_apply_count,h.successful_loan_count,h.total_remit_amt,
 h.early_settle_count,h.late_settle_count,h.max_past_late_days,
 (e.vir_date-h.registration_date)::float8 AS user_age_days,
 h.apply_amt_l1,h.apply_amt_l2,h.apply_amt_l3,
 h.apply_util_l1,h.apply_util_l2,h.apply_util_l3,
 h.apply_gap_l1,h.apply_gap_l2,h.apply_gap_l3,
 h.remit_amt_l1,h.remit_amt_l2,h.remit_amt_l3,
 EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (e.vir_time-h.last_apply_time))/86400.0 AS days_since_last_apply,
 EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (e.vir_time-h.last_remit_time))/86400.0 AS days_since_last_remit,
 ch.credit_event_count,ch.credit_pass_count,
 ch.credit_amt_l1,ch.credit_amt_l2,ch.credit_amt_l3
FROM tmp_t0ml_events e
JOIN tmp_t0ml_orders p ON p.user_id=e.user_id AND p.serial_id=e.settled_serial_id
LEFT JOIN tmp_t0ml_order_credit c ON c.user_id=p.user_id AND c.serial_id=p.serial_id
LEFT JOIN wangchuanliang.model_result_copy m ON m.serial_id=e.serial_id AND m.user_id=e.user_id
LEFT JOIN tmp_t0ml_order_history h ON h.event_id=e.serial_id
LEFT JOIN tmp_t0ml_credit_history ch ON ch.event_id=e.serial_id
)
SELECT * FROM tmp_t0ml_features ORDER BY event_id;
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/history_offers.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
WITH chosen AS (
 SELECT v.user_id,v.serial_id,v.vir_date,v.vir_unix,v.is_pass1,v.loan_type_code,
 v.max_credit_apply_amt,v.risk_product
 FROM wangchuanliang.order_vir_f_copy v
 WHERE v.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e') AND v.is_pass1=1
 AND v.vir_unix>0 AND v.vir_unix<%(cutoff_unix)s
), recycle AS (
 SELECT r.serial_id,
 MAX(CASE WHEN r.recycle_type=2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS has_type2,
 MAX(CASE WHEN r.recycle_type=3 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS has_type3,
 COUNT(DISTINCT r.recycle_type) AS type_distinct_n
 FROM wangchuanliang.side_recycle_type_copy r
 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM chosen c WHERE c.serial_id=r.serial_id)
 GROUP BY r.serial_id
)
SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY v.user_id,v.vir_unix,v.serial_id) AS export_id,
 v.user_id::bigint,v.serial_id::bigint,v.vir_date::date,
 (TO_TIMESTAMP(v.vir_unix) AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize') AS credit_time,
 v.is_pass1,v.loan_type_code,v.max_credit_apply_amt::float8 AS credit_amt,
 COALESCE(r.has_type2,0) AS has_type2,COALESCE(r.has_type3,0) AS has_type3,
 COALESCE(r.type_distinct_n,0) AS type_distinct_n
FROM chosen v LEFT JOIN recycle r ON r.serial_id=v.serial_id
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/new_users_daily_202401.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_fed0257e3aa18658
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2024-01-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2024-02-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/new_users_daily_202401_example.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_fed0257e3aa18658
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2024-01-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2024-02-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/new_users_daily_202402.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_fed0257e3aa18658
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2024-02-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2024-03-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/new_users_daily_202403.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_fed0257e3aa18658
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2024-03-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2024-04-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');

t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/new_users_daily_202410.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_fed0257e3aa18658
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT e.user_id,e.create_date,COUNT(e.user_id)::float8 AS events,MAX(e.create_time) AS last_active_time,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_in' AND e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS newpage,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('amount_modification','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new','loan_form_loan_amount_selectionn_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS amount,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_contract_newdetails','Loan_contract_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS contract,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('loan_form_term_selection_new','loan_form_term_selection_new_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS term,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newconfirm','Loan_newconfirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_plan_newdetails','Loan_plan_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS plan,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_purpose_newdetails','Loan_purpose_newdetails_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS purpose,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_back','Order_coupons_back_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_back,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Loan_newpage','Loan_newpage_select') THEN CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS apply_duration
FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2024-10-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2024-11-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');

SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_confirm','Order_coupons_confirm_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_confirm,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.props_id IN ('Order_coupons_page','Order_coupons_page_select') THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS coupon_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(e.props_id,'')||' '||COALESCE(e.page_name,'')) LIKE '%repay%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS repayment_page,
SUM(CASE WHEN e.event_type='page_view_out' AND e.event_end_time>=e.event_start_time AND e.event_end_time<=EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM((e.create_date+1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'))*1000 THEN LEAST((e.event_end_time-e.event_start_time)/1000.0,3600.0) ELSE 0 END)::float8 AS duration,

FROM wangchuanliang.event_track_record e
WHERE e.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658')
AND e.create_date>=DATE '2026-06-01' AND e.create_date<DATE '2026-07-01'
GROUP BY e.user_id,e.create_date
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_fed0257e3aa18658');

t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/orders.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT DISTINCT ON(o.user_id,o.serial_id)
 o.serial_id::bigint AS serial_id,o.user_id::bigint AS user_id,
 o.reg_date,o.apply_time,o.apply_date,o.apply_amt::float8,
 o.remit_time,o.remit_date,o.remit_amt::float8,o.is_remit,
 o.repaid_time,o.repaid_date,o.is_repaid,o.loan_status_code,
 o.due_date,o.total_term::float8,o.loan_day::float8,
 o.pre_amt::float8,o.post_amt::float8,o.remit_succ_rnb,o.product_no,o.loan_type
FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy o
WHERE o.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e')
AND o.apply_time<TIMESTAMP '2026-10-25 00:00:00'
ORDER BY o.user_id,o.serial_id,o.apply_time
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/payments.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入批次：users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY r.user_id,r.payin_date,r.payin_time,r.serial_id) AS export_id,
 r.user_id::bigint,r.serial_id::bigint,r.payin_date::date,r.payin_time,
 SUM(r.amount)::float8 AS amount,COUNT(*) AS source_rows
FROM wangchuanliang.repayment_record_copy r
WHERE r.user_id IN (SELECT user_id FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e')
AND r.payin_date<DATE '2026-10-25' AND r.amount>0
GROUP BY r.user_id,r.serial_id,r.payin_date,r.payin_time
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_users WHERE batch_key = 'users_5bd6a6ffe75fbf2e');
t0_v4_business_reliability_20261026/release_sql/t0_v4_validation_20261024_20261025/sql/product_profiles_daily.sql
/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。
 * 依赖下方batch_key对应的冻结授权输入；各批次不能混用。
 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT remit_date::date AS profile_date,product_no::text AS product_no,
       COUNT(*) AS orders,SUM(remit_amt)::float8 AS remit_sum,
       SUM(pre_amt+post_amt)::float8 AS fee_sum,
       SUM(loan_day)::float8 AS day_sum,COUNT(loan_day) AS day_n,
       SUM(total_term)::float8 AS term_sum,COUNT(total_term) AS term_n
FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy
WHERE is_remit=1 AND remit_amt>0
  AND remit_time>TIMESTAMP '2000-01-01'
  AND remit_time<TIMESTAMP '2026-10-25 00:00:00'
  AND remit_date<DATE '2026-10-25'
  AND pre_amt IS NOT NULL AND post_amt IS NOT NULL AND loan_day>0
  AND product_no IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY remit_date::date,product_no::text
ORDER BY product_no,profile_date;

/* 发布副本：原授权SQL保持只读；本文件不内嵌业务用户/订单ID。

 * 输入为空时返回空结果，绝不自动移除范围限制。详见00_发布SQL输入表说明.txt。
 */
SELECT release_result.*
FROM (
SELECT COUNT(*) AS matched_source_rows,COUNT(DISTINCT serial_id) AS matched_distinct_event_orders,SUM(CASE WHEN kabybxgboost405apd7crecyc IS NOT NULL AND kabybxgboost405apd7crecyc<>-9999999 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS valid_405,SUM(CASE WHEN kabybxgboost363dpd7cre IS NOT NULL AND kabybxgboost363dpd7cre<>-9999999 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS valid_363 FROM wangchuanliang.model_result_copy WHERE serial_id IN (SELECT credit_serial_id FROM validation_target_offers WHERE batch_key = 'offers_d4fdf4ccd2a65709')
) release_result
WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM validation_target_offers WHERE batch_key = 'offers_d4fdf4ccd2a65709');
